- id: computer-use-human-visible-actions
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: feature/computer-use-human-visible
  task_type: feature/computer-use-target-bound-visual-pointer
  depends_on: user clarification on 2026-09-19 CST: Computer Use should use a visible mouse/click/input-style experience for both foreground and background targets, but must not bring a background app to the foreground by default or interfere with the user's current app.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseOverlayBridge.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseOverlay.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseMacNative.swift`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, Computer Use overlay/native tests, and docs/skill text if behavior changes.
  base_commit: 5a7f9772394762708c0be3b7b7f49d08c61f37ac
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Add target-bound visible pointer/click/input visualization for Computer Use so foreground and background targets both show a mouse-like action process, while background execution remains non-disruptive and does not activate the target by default.
  design_intent: The user wants a human-readable visual process everywhere: a proxy pointer moves to the target element, clicks/presses, and shows input/typing proof. For background targets the native action body must remain target-bound semantic AX (for example `setText`/`pressText`) and must not use background coordinate CGEvents or steal focus; the visual layer should be a target-bound proxy/replay/proof, not a claim that the macOS system cursor clicked a hidden window. For foreground targets or explicit foreground fallback, real system cursor/native input may be used after activation/reobserve/frontmost gates.
  product_contract: Default behavior should not activate a background app. Both foreground and background actions should produce visible target-bound pointer/action feedback. If the target is frontmost/visible, the user should see the proxy pointer/click/input on the target window. If the target is background/occluded, the system must not draw a fake cursor over the user's current foreground app; instead it should show Morpheus-side proof such as target-window crop replay/highlight/action card, and ideally allow recent action replay/highlight when the user later switches to the target app. Audit must distinguish `backgroundAX` native execution from `foregroundRealInput`, and distinguish proxy visualization from real system cursor movement.
  invariants: Do not weaken the foreground gate for coordinate mouse/keyboard actions. Do not send background CGEvent mouse/keyboard. Do not move the macOS system cursor for background AX success. Do not default-activate background targets. Do not draw background target pointer paths over unrelated foreground app coordinates. Do not make plain `move` silently change semantics for existing callers. Do not hide text-risk confirmation or pasteboard restore guarantees. Do not log sensitive full typed text beyond current bounded audit policy.
  forbidden_paths: no silent background AX for the new visible policy; no fake overlay over the current foreground app for a background target; no claiming background AX was a real system mouse click; no background coordinate click/type; no bypass of native helper/TCC subject; no ChatGPT-specific special case; no default focus stealing just to make the animation visible.
  expected_implementation_outline: Prefer a model-facing policy/mode with names like `preferBackgroundWithTargetVisualization`, `visiblePointerMode: "targetBound"`, and explicit evidence fields such as `nativeExecution: "backgroundAX" | "foregroundRealInput"`, `visualization: "targetBoundProxy" | "morpheusProof"`, `systemCursorMoved: boolean`. For background `setText`/`pressText` success, use matched element bounds/center to emit proxy cursor path, click/press/type visual event, and element highlight/action proof. If the existing desktop overlay cannot safely render for background targets, add/use a Morpheus-side proof/crop replay path rather than drawing over the wrong app. For foreground target or explicit fallback, activation/reobserve/frontmost gate may lead to real system cursor move/click/type; if native bridge currently clicks without moving the system cursor, add bounded mouse-move evidence while preserving that plain `move` is agent-cursor-only. Owner should clearly state whether this tranche supports later replay when the user switches to the background app; if not, document the architecture gap and next tranche.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Background `setText`/`pressText` success produces visible target-bound proof/replay/highlight and does not activate target or move system cursor; proof evidence says semantic AX/backgroundAX, not real mouse click; background proof never draws over unrelated foreground app coordinates; foreground/frontmost target shows pointer/click/input visualization and, when using real input, records system cursor movement; background no-match/multiple/truncated/unsupported/native-failure only transitions to foreground real-input fallback when the selected policy allows it; fallback trace includes background failure reason plus foreground real-input evidence; strict background calls can still fail without foreground fallback; MCP and CLI schemas/help expose the policy/mode; stop cleans proof/overlay; focused JS/MCP/CLI tests pass; Swift compile passes if native changed; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.

- id: computer-use-mcp-overlay-parity
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/computer-use-mcp-overlay-parity
  task_type: bugfix/computer-use-mcp-feature-parity
  depends_on: user validation on 2026-09-18 CST: MCP Computer Use can perform native actions through the stable LaunchServices socket service, but the user-visible cursor/trajectory that existed in the CLI/Electron-main path is absent; MCP action hotkey format also rejected natural/CLI-style `cmd+a` attempts.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/main.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseOverlay.cjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs`, tests around Computer Use manager/MCP/overlay, and type definitions if an IPC bridge is added.
  base_commit: 0f394c770e496d91dced785b71bf84a8c61d2b1f
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Restore full Computer Use feature parity for the model-facing MCP path: visible agent cursor/trajectory overlay, cleanup on stop, and hotkey action compatibility must work when actions are invoked through `mcp__computer_use__`, not only through the developer CLI.
  design_intent: The stable helper/socket service should remain the actual macOS TCC/native execution subject, but user-visible feedback belongs to the running Morpheus Electron UI. Moving native execution to MCP must not remove the previous visible cursor affordance; MCP actions should emit/bridge cursor state to the Electron overlay controller while preserving honest audit evidence and target-bound safety.
  current_problem_model: MCP helper sessions currently create/use Computer Use manager state outside the Electron main process, so `overlayController` is absent and actions return `Computer Use overlay controller is unavailable for this session.` The native service executes click/type/key correctly, but `move` is only audit/path evidence and no visible overlay is drawn. The `hotkey` action normalizer requires separate `key` plus `modifiers`; natural CLI-style input such as `cmd+a` or `{keys:["cmd","a"]}` is rejected, producing a feature mismatch between CLI shorthand and MCP schema.
  invariants: Keep native AX/ScreenCapture/input execution inside the stable LaunchServices service socket path. Do not regress TCC identity, stale payload identity checks, socket ownership validation, MCP stdio discovery, or honest diagnostics. Do not move native desktop control into the UI process. Do not draw overlay over the wrong target/window; retain target-bound/background visibility rules. Stop/session cleanup must remove visible overlay. Clipboard restore guarantees for `type` must remain.
  forbidden_paths: no fake visible cursor in tool output only; no moving the real macOS system cursor just to make movement visible; no direct-exec helper fallback as the stable path; no bypass of service socket identity; no broad global overlay that ignores target visibility/bounds; no weakening high-risk shortcut policy; no app-server hardcoded fake Computer Use tools.
  expected_implementation_outline: Prefer a small provider-neutral bridge from MCP Computer Use session state/events back to the currently running Electron main overlay controller, so MCP `move`/pointer-path/action updates can call the same `createComputerUseOverlayController` path used by the CLI/Electron manager. If direct in-process sharing is impossible because MCP runs in a helper process, add an explicit local IPC/event channel owned by the Morpheus runtime/app-server/Electron main and consumed only for overlay rendering; helper/native service remains responsible for native execution. Normalize hotkey actions to accept both explicit `{key:"a", modifiers:["cmd"]}` and CLI-like `{key:"cmd+a"}` / `{keys:["cmd","a"]}` where safe. Add typed state/audit evidence showing overlay availability/visibility and preserving existing `agentCursor`/`pointerPath`.
  minimum_regression_matrix: MCP `start_session` on the current Root Worker app returns permissions OK and overlay availability when frontmost; MCP `move` makes overlay visible and records path; MCP `click`/`drag` update visible cursor feedback without leaving the system cursor displaced; MCP `stop` destroys/hides overlay; background or bounds-missing target keeps overlay hidden with explicit reason; MCP hotkey accepts `cmd+a` style and explicit modifiers while preserving high-risk policy for destructive shortcuts; existing Computer Use JS tests/MCP tests pass; Swift compile and RMCP helper test still pass; app-server debug build if runtime IPC path changes; PM installed validation must prove the visible cursor is observable through the current client after full Capsule restart.
  next_action: none.
  blockers: none.

  base_commit: 519a44b873e27e695ba2c28ca9aec2cc571dc950
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Provide a stable `/Applications` Computer Use helper app path for installed macOS builds so macOS Screen Recording/Accessibility authorization does not drift with every Runtime Capsule release path.
  design_intent: Users should authorize one stable Computer Use app identity, not a release-local helper nested under `MORPHEUS_HOME/runtime-launcher/artifacts/<release-id>/...`. Runtime Capsule releases may continue to contain the canonical helper resources, but installed/default MCP config should use a stable external helper app path once it is installed or refreshed safely.
  current_problem_model: The current packaged helper bundle path lives inside the active Runtime Capsule release directory. Each full Runtime Capsule rebuild changes the release id and therefore the helper app path, which can cause macOS TCC permission prompts/trust to attach to a path that disappears or changes after restart. User asked whether placing the app in Applications would avoid that.
  invariants: Keep model-facing Computer Use as config-discovered MCP tools. Preserve honest diagnostics for packaged helper/native subject and permissions. Do not move desktop control into Launcher. Do not require manual copy as the product fix. Do not overwrite unrelated user apps or broaden destructive file operations. Keep release-local helper as source of truth/update candidate and keep dev fallback behavior explicit.
  forbidden_paths: no one-off manual copy without startup/install/update integration; no hardcoded fake `stablePermissionSubject:true`; no asking users to authorize release-local artifact path as final UX; no deleting/replacing arbitrary `/Applications` entries without identity/version checks; no weakening MCP env isolation; no built-in tool injection workaround.
  expected_implementation_outline: Add or reuse an installed macOS helper app materialization step that copies/updates `Root Worker Computer Use.app` from the active Runtime Capsule resources to a stable path such as `/Applications/Root Worker Computer Use.app` when safe. Prefer atomic/staged replacement and identity/version/source checks. Update packaged/default Computer Use MCP config/env so installed mode launches the stable Applications helper path, while falling back to release-local helper only when the stable app is unavailable. Diagnostics should report the effective helper app path and whether the permission subject is stable. Tests should prove generated config points at the stable helper path/variable and that fallback semantics remain honest.
  minimum_regression_matrix: packaged/default config uses stable helper path or stable env var; stable helper materialization/update is tested without touching real `/Applications`; release-local fallback remains available and diagnostically distinct; `permissions_status` reports effective helper path and stable subject truthfully; MCP tools list/call still work; existing Computer Use/package tests pass; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.

  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer

  branch: feature/computer-use-socket-service
  task_type: feature/computer-use-macos-service-transport
  depends_on: user follow-up on 2026-09-18 CST asking why not make the stable Computer Use app itself the MCP/socket service; baseline canonical main `495c05e71ce796c381a74047dfc59d496d2960a0`; installed release `sha256:16d4dd5927270c9901318c278849232ceab3ab7b73b1bb0f21c6714636c8d339` already proves LaunchServices app-host TCC works.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseMacNative.swift`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/installedArtifactUpdate.cjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs`, Computer Use tests, RMCP helper tests, and package/config tests.
  base_commit: 495c05e71ce796c381a74047dfc59d496d2960a0
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Replace the per-call LaunchServices file-IPC bridge with a stable `/Applications/Root Worker Computer Use.app` LaunchServices-hosted service using socket/local IPC, so the authorized app is the long-lived native operation host while Morpheus still exposes Computer Use as config-discovered MCP tools.
  design_intent: The verified permission boundary should become the actual service boundary. The stable helper app should be the authoritative macOS TCC subject and native operation executor, not a short-lived per-command process behind a stdio MCP shim. Keep MCP as the model/runtime tool contract, but move native command execution to a long-lived LaunchServices app-host service reached over an explicit local IPC transport.
  current_problem_model: Current installed code works but has an architectural seam: app-server starts a direct-exec MCP stdio process for protocol/tool inventory, and each native observe/action launches `open -n -a ... ipc-command` with request/response files. This proved TCC semantics but is slower, creates many app-host processes, and leaves lifecycle/reconnect/version readiness implicit. User correctly asked why not use socket MCP or a service hosted by the Computer Use app itself.
  invariants: Preserve installed TCC result: true native AX/ScreenCapture calls must happen inside a LaunchServices-launched `/Applications/Root Worker Computer Use.app` process. Preserve model-facing Computer Use as config-discovered MCP tools unless the generic runtime gains an explicit socket MCP transport in-scope. Preserve stable path and honest diagnostics. Keep release-local fallback truthful. Do not weaken MCP env isolation. Do not move desktop control into Launcher/runtime payload. Do not require users to authorize release-local artifacts again.
  forbidden_paths: no regression to direct-exec AX calls; no hardcoded/fake `stablePermissionSubject:true`; no global sleeps/polling loops without bounded timeout and stale cleanup; no unauthenticated broad TCP listener; no world-writable socket without ownership checks; no stale service continuing to use an old payload after Runtime Capsule update; no app-server special-case fake tools; no destructive replacement of unrelated `/Applications` apps.
  expected_implementation_outline: Prefer a minimal local Unix-domain socket service hosted by the stable helper app. Add a LaunchServices `service`/default app-host mode that starts or reuses one service instance, binds a per-user socket under a safe runtime dir, writes a ready/version file if needed, validates that the service points at the active payload/release, and serves typed requests for observe/action/screenshot. Keep the existing stdio MCP server as a thin adapter initially if adding generic socket MCP transport is too large: MCP tool calls should connect to the stable service socket instead of per-call `open -n` file IPC. If practical and bounded, add an explicit `socket` MCP/server mode or config knob, but do not block on full generic MCP transport if a thin adapter is safer for this tranche. Add service discovery/start/reconnect logic with bounded launch via `open -a`, stale socket cleanup, single-instance behavior, and diagnostics reporting service transport/status/version. Update tests to cover service startup, socket request/response, stale socket/version mismatch fallback, and no direct-exec stable overclaim.
  minimum_regression_matrix: stable helper MCP tools still list six tools; `permissions_status(includeObservation:true)` reports `nativeInvocationMode`/transport as service/socket, stable subject true, Screen Recording granted, Accessibility true after install; observe/action requests go through LaunchServices service socket rather than per-call file IPC; service restart/reconnect works after process exit; stale socket or release mismatch is detected and relaunched; release-local fallback remains honest; package config/env still safe; existing Computer Use JS tests, MCP tests, RMCP helper tests, Swift compile, app-server debug build, and `git diff --check` pass; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required after merge because this changes installed macOS Computer Use service/lifecycle behavior; PM must build full Runtime Capsule, restart, verify active release, self-debug, service process/socket identity, MCP inventory/tools, and installed permission/observe smoke.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM confirmed dev-3 was clean, created branch `feature/computer-use-socket-service` from canonical main `495c05e7`, and dispatched to owner_dev_3. Owner delivered `d9d47635c9` with fixed reviewer approval and validation pass. PM design inspection found a blocker: service identity only compared bundle/executable/protocolVersion, while Swift `serviceIdentity()` returned `payloadElectronPath` but `serviceIdentityMatches()` ignored it. This would allow a stale stable helper service from an older Runtime Capsule to keep serving the same socket after an update because the stable bundle/executable path remains unchanged. PM requested返工. Owner delivered `9f28820e16`: JS expected identity now includes `payloadElectronPath` from current MCP adapter `process.execPath`, JS preflights every real command with status and independently compares service identity before sending observe/action/screenshot, Swift service also compares `payloadElectronPath`, launch-time socket dir prep now uses `lstat` and refuses symlink/non-owned/group-world-accessible dirs, and tests cover stale payload service shutdown/relaunch without receiving real observe. Reviewer re-approved. PM design inspection accepted and merged to canonical main as `0f394c770322c39e527d29cffc8527d843282ac1` via `Merge Computer Use socket service host`; main focused validation passed.

- id: computer-use-mcp-standard-stdio
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/computer-use-standard-mcp
  task_type: bugfix/mcp-runtime-injection
  depends_on: user report on 2026-09-18 CST that direct `mcp__computer_use__` tool was not injected after registration; canonical main `76a735601161a9b81f056697ff7c34e647f74cf7`; dev-3 fast-forwarded to that commit.
  files: likely `scripts/morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs`, its tests, packaging/helper launcher tests, and possibly default config smoke tests. Avoid changing generic RMCP client unless evidence shows the generic client is wrong.
  base_commit: 76a735601161a9b81f056697ff7c34e647f74cf7
  status:installed_effective
  objective: Make the packaged Computer Use MCP server usable by Morpheus' generic RMCP stdio client so `computer_use` contributes tools to app-server MCP inventory and model-visible `mcp__computer_use__*` tools are injected normally.
  design_intent: The product contract is config-discovered MCP tools, not shell/CLI/manual JSON-RPC. Packaged helper and native permission subject are already valid; the remaining break is the MCP transport contract between app-server's RMCP client and the helper server.
  current_problem_model: App-server `mcpServerStatus/list` on ws port 8919 shows `computer_use` exists but `authStatus:"unsupported"` and `tools:{}`. Directly launching the packaged helper with newline-delimited JSON works and returns six tools plus stable packaged diagnostics. A previous manual Content-Length request timed out. `scripts/morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs` uses `readline` NDJSON framing, while generic MCP startup goes through `codex_rmcp_client` / RMCP stdio transport. This strongly indicates Computer Use helper is not speaking the standard MCP stdio framing expected by the runtime, so inventory cannot initialize it.
  invariants: Keep model-facing Computer Use as configured MCP tools. Keep the stable packaged helper/native executable permission subject and honest diagnostics. Do not add special-case built-in Computer Use tools or tool_search hacks. Do not change global RMCP framing to accommodate only this helper. Existing NDJSON debug smoke may remain only if clearly separated from standard MCP mode. No destructive desktop actions in validation.
  forbidden_paths: no manual shell CLI as final interface; no hardcoded `mcp__computer_use__` injection; no marking `stablePermissionSubject:true` unless packaged native executable remains actual native subject; no generic RMCP client downgrade to non-standard line JSON; no app-server status fake tools without a real initialized MCP client.
  expected_implementation_outline: Prefer making `morpheus-computer-use-mcp.mjs` support the standard MCP stdio transport used by RMCP, either by implementing Content-Length framing or by using an MCP SDK/server transport compatible with runtime expectations. If keeping NDJSON for developer smoke, make standard framing the default for configured MCP or add an explicit mode selected by the packaged launcher/config. Update tests to prove standard framed initialize + tools/list + permissions_status works through the same command configured in `[mcp_servers.computer_use]`. Add/adjust package tests so the installed helper launcher starts standard MCP. If practical, add an app-server/RMCP integration test with this helper or a minimal equivalent framing server.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Direct standard MCP stdio smoke of packaged helper returns six tools; app-server `mcpServerStatus/list` reports `computer_use` with tools populated and not `unsupported`; `tool_search`/model dynamic tool exposure can discover `computer_use` after refresh/new turn; NDJSON developer path either still passes or is intentionally deprecated with docs/tests updated; `computer.permissions_status(includeObservation:false)` still reports packaged stable native subject; existing Computer Use MCP/package tests pass; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required after merge because this changes installed model-visible tool injection behavior. PM must build full Runtime Capsule, request restart, then verify app-server MCP status and direct model-visible tool invocation if exposed.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM confirmed config exists in user and project config, app-server inventory contains `computer_use` but no tools/auth (`unsupported` fallback), packaged helper paths/env exist, and direct NDJSON helper smoke works.   current_problem_model: Installed release `31c3bdfd` contains `Root Worker Computer Use.app` and MCP server. Direct MCP smoke works, but `permissions_status` reports `nativeControlSubject: delegated-swift-script-and-screencapture` and `stablePermissionSubject:false`. In `computerUse.cjs`, `createMacNativeComputerUseClient` runs AX commands with `/usr/bin/swift <computerUseMacNative.swift>` and screenshots with `/usr/sbin/screencapture`, so macOS TCC prompts/trust may attach to delegated toolchain processes rather than the helper bundle.

Launcher stays simple and does not own desktop control. `ComputerUseManager` continues to enforce risk policy, confirmation, target evidence, audit, no fake success, and no high-risk action without permission. No destructive click/type smoke in PM validation. Diagnostics must distinguish packaged helper identity, native control subject, Screen Recording, Accessibility, and limitations honestly. Existing Electron UI Computer Use IPC must not regress.

Options are open if validated: compile the existing Swift source to an executable during packaging and have MCP/helper call that executable; or make the helper executable itself a native binary that hosts stdio MCP or a local protocol and performs native operations directly. The important closure is that native AX and screenshot APIs are invoked by the stable helper bundle/executable, not by `/usr/bin/swift`, `screencapture`, or payload Electron/node. Update `computerUse.cjs` native client abstraction so packaged mode uses the native helper executable/protocol and dev mode can keep Swift script fallback clearly marked. Update MCP `permissions_status` so `stablePermissionSubject:true` only when packaged native subject is actually used; otherwise keep limitations. Extend packaging/signing/tests accordingly.

- id: live-subscribed-init-context-items
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/live-subscribed-init-context-items
  task_type: bugfix/live-subscription-display-transport
  depends_on: user report on 2026-09-18 CST that after restart init context becomes visible, but live subscribed item transport still appears broken; canonical main `a3800e76d21016bcf2d520241a9ee68077e7bce4`; dev-3 fast-forwarded to that commit.
  files: likely backend event/display/history projection and notification transport around thread subscribed items, preload IPC subscription handling, frontend conversation merge/render paths, compact/init context typed display item code, tests for live vs read/reload paths.
  base_commit: a3800e76d21016bcf2d520241a9ee68077e7bce4
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Fix live subscribed item delivery/display so compact summary and init context/provider-visible context items appear in the active conversation through the live subscription path, without requiring app restart or full thread reload.
  design_intent: A thread item that is persisted and visible after restart/read must also reach currently subscribed UI clients through the live event path. Compact summary should be a normal visible message/typed item, not hidden behind a collapsed summary-only UI. Init context / project instructions / provider-visible reinjection should have durable typed display evidence when it is allowed to be shown, so UI transparency does not depend on restart/reload.
  current_problem_model: User observed init context was not visible in UI before restart, then appeared after restart. That strongly suggests the item existed in persisted history or replay projection, but live subscription delivery, frontend merge, invalidation, or item-type render filtering failed for the running subscribed view. The issue may affect compact summary, init context, or other system/provider-visible typed items differently from ordinary assistant/user messages.
  invariants: Do not fix by forcing full `thread/read(includeTurns=true)` after every item or after compact. Do not use raw marker parsing or frontend-only fake rows. Do not leak hidden system/developer instructions; distinguish displayable user/project init context and provider-visible injected content from non-displayable privileged context. Preserve typed `EventMsg -> ThreadItem` display architecture and reload behavior. Background subscribed threads should receive live display items via the same subscription contract as active threads. Existing message/tool/command rows must not duplicate when a later read snapshot arrives.
  forbidden_paths: no path-specific `/self` or compact-only special case if a generic typed item transport rule is missing; no CSS-only uncollapse; no unbounded polling; no relying on localStorage refresh; no hiding the issue behind restart/reload; no changing model-visible context without matching typed display/audit evidence where transparency requires it.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with a focused test that appends/projects an init-context or compact-summary display item while a frontend/client is subscribed, and show that the live view receives the same typed item that a later thread/read/reload returns. Inspect backend notification targeting (`ThreadScopedOutgoingMessageSender`, subscribed connection ids, item append/projection events), wire serialization, preload subscription bridge, frontend store merge/dedupe, and render filtering for system/init/summary typed items. Fix the narrow broken layer so live and persisted projections use the same item shape and ids. Add regression tests for active subscribed thread, background subscribed thread then selection, and reload/read dedupe.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Live subscribed active thread receives init context item without restart; live subscribed active thread receives compact summary as a visible message/typed item and not only collapsed metadata; background subscribed thread receives item and shows it on selection without full read; later `thread/read` does not duplicate the item; non-displayable hidden system/developer context is not leaked; existing user/assistant/tool/command subscription tests still pass; frontend conversation render tests cover the item type; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered in Runtime Capsule artifact `sha256:31c3bdfdc3b09cc74b2389083262fae755d8d28b6672de64a0a158e737d1650a` via restart request `call_4jT8bluvJZdcEmzQtQ5VwYQd`.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM recorded user report and prepared dev-3 branch `fix/live-subscribed-init-context-items` from canonical main `a3800e76d21016bcf2d520241a9ee68077e7bce4`; dispatched full brief to `/self/owner_dev_3`, requiring end-to-end live subscribed item transport fix and fixed reviewer approval. Owner delivered commit `de24c99e5`: backend `thread/start` now sends live `item/completed` notifications for typed `ThreadItem::InjectedContext` already present in the start snapshot, after `thread/started`; native/project/external root starts are covered; fork copied historical injected context is not replayed. Owner/reviewer validation passed for precise related tests and app-server build; broad `thread_start` filter still has two reported existing/environment-related failures, and repo-wide `cargo fmt --check` has existing unrelated formatting failures. PM reviewed diff and accepted design alignment: no raw hidden context read, no full-read workaround, external root covered, fork copied historical context not replayed. PM merged to canonical main as `930141fc1683e68e76176a877024b3967c301e42` via `Merge live startup init context notifications`. PM main validation passed: exact external/native/project start regressions, exact fork non-replay regression, app-server debug build, and `git diff --check HEAD~1..HEAD`; existing linker warning noted. PM clean full mac package succeeded after a long release LTO build; seed release was `sha256:31c3bdfdc3b09cc74b2389083262fae755d8d28b6672de64a0a158e737d1650a`, sourceCommit `930141fc1683e68e76176a877024b3967c301e42`. Restart request `call_4jT8bluvJZdcEmzQtQ5VwYQd` completed; control state selected/active external release is `31c3bdfd` with payload pid `11765`. Self-debug CDP attached to active renderer at `31c3bdfd`, console 0 errors/0 warnings, current conversation showed the runtime restart recovered live item and live command item. Backend focused tests remain the primary hard evidence for live startup injected context because UI `createThread` also merges start response snapshot. dev-3 was fast-forwarded to main merge `930141fc`.

- id: computer-use-mcp-helper
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: feature/computer-use-mcp-helper
  task_type: feature/computer-use-model-tool-surface-and-stable-permission-helper
  depends_on: user request on 2026-09-18 CST to directly implement Computer Use exposure as MCP/tool rather than model shell CLI; current installed app bundle has launcher commit `8d6ca36b3a78d0552654a706f6f18f4e2d2bb9a9`; dev-3 fast-forwarded to canonical main `8d6ca36b3a78d0552654a706f6f18f4e2d2bb9a9`.
  files: likely `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, Electron main/preload Computer Use IPC, MCP config/default tool registration surfaces, app-server MCP dynamic tools/config docs/tests, packaging scripts for a stable helper if feasible in first tranche.
  base_commit: 8d6ca36b3a78d0552654a706f6f18f4e2d2bb9a9
  status: installed_effective_packaged_helper_tranche
  objective: Make Computer Use available to models through a configured MCP/tool surface backed by a stable Computer Use helper identity, instead of requiring models to execute shell CLI commands. Keep the CLI as a developer/debug client, not the primary model interface.
  design_intent: Computer Use is a high-side-effect desktop capability and must have a typed model-visible protocol, stable authorization identity, durable audit/evidence, and session lifecycle. The current CLI/repl is useful for smoke tests but inherits the runtime/app-server/node process identity, so macOS Accessibility permission remains confusing even when the user authorizes the visible Prototype app. The first product direction is a helper-as-MCP-server architecture: a stable helper owns Screen Recording/Accessibility/native actions and exposes MCP tools; Morpheus loads it through configuration/dynamic MCP rather than hardcoding bespoke model tools in app-server.
  current_problem_model: Smoke after installing launcher commit showed Screen Recording can work, but Computer Use action is blocked by `accessibilityTrusted:false`. Actual target/foreground app is payload `Root Worker Runtime` (`com.openai.root-worker-prototype.runtime.dev`), while user naturally authorized outer `Root Worker Prototype` (`com.openai.root-worker-prototype.dev`). Current `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs repl` is only a long-lived Node session with `operationBoundary=computer-use-repl-session`, not an independent `.app` permission subject. Electron renderer IPC also keeps ComputerUseManager inside the payload app. Therefore user-facing permission and model exposure should move to a stable helper/MCP boundary rather than asking the model to run CLI commands.
  invariants: Launcher remains simple and should not own desktop control. Do not expose Computer Use to models as ad-hoc shell commands. Do not hardcode Computer Use built-in tools directly into app-server if existing MCP dynamic tool loading can provide the tool surface. CLI must remain available for local debugging, but should become a thin client or share the same server contract where feasible. High-risk actions still require typed risk policy/audit and must not silently click/type without target/permission evidence. Existing Electron UI Computer Use IPC/tests should not regress. Do not fake Accessibility trust or treat screenshot-only observation as permission to control.
  forbidden_paths: no model-facing `exec_command` wrapper as the final tool interface; no requiring users to authorize every ephemeral payload/node path; no moving desktop control into the Launcher process; no broad hidden global permission bypass; no path-specific hack for current `64a000...` artifact; no hardcoded MCP tool list in app-server if it can be config-driven; no destructive click/type smoke in validation.
  expected_implementation_outline: First map existing MCP server config/dynamic tools and existing ComputerUseManager/CLI boundaries. Implement a minimal first tranche that introduces a configured Computer Use MCP server/helper contract. If a fully packaged `.app` helper is too large for one tranche, create the stable helper/server entrypoint and config plumbing now, with explicit TODO/non-goals for packaging, but do not leave model exposure as shell CLI. Prefer tools such as `computer.start_session`, `computer.observe`, `computer.find_text`, `computer.act`, `computer.stop`, and `computer.permissions_status`. The helper/MCP side should call the existing ComputerUseManager/native bridge, preserve session/audit policy, and return typed observation/action results. Update CLI to use the shared server contract or keep direct mode clearly marked as debug/fallback. Add permission diagnostics that report actual bundle id/path requiring Accessibility (`com.openai.root-worker-prototype.runtime.dev` today, future stable helper id when packaged). Add tests for MCP tool schemas/results/policy blocking without performing real desktop side effects.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Unit tests for MCP tool schema/list/call results; permission status reports bundle id/path and distinguishes Screen Recording vs Accessibility; `observe` returns typed screenshot/target evidence without AX elements when Accessibility is missing; `find_text`/`act` are policy-blocked when `accessibilityTrusted=false`; CLI remains functional for debug run/repl; existing `computerUse.test.cjs` and relevant Electron preload/main tests pass; app-server/MCP dynamic tool tests pass if touched; package/build impact understood; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.

  depends_on: user request on 2026-09-18 CST to submit existing Launcher changes; canonical main `76921248489aa457bd93f7d5afa34c1494daacf9`; installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:64a000b0b7350e6fff65d655a53e60e7e9401fd3a199519d435883bf9efb9e55`.
  files: `codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/capsule.rs`, `codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/control.rs`, `codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/migration.rs`, `codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/supervisor.rs`.
  base_commit: 76921248489aa457bd93f7d5afa34c1494daacf9
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Review, validate, and commit the existing runtime-launcher change that treats a missing prior payload root as a diagnostic stale launch record instead of attempting unsafe descendant cleanup that can keep launcher recovery blocked.
  design_intent: Launcher reconciliation must prefer safe recovery over unsafe signaling. If the recorded payload root no longer exists, persisted descendant pids are no longer a trustworthy process tree authority; the launcher should discard the stale active launch, record bounded failure evidence, and continue selection/spawn rather than sending signals to ambiguous descendants or leaving a sticky launch gate.
  current_problem_model: Current dirty diff in `supervisor.rs` changes `reconcile_active_launch` for `resolve_identity(payload.payload.pid) == None`: previously it called `cleanup_payload(... known_descendants ...)` and could block/error on stale descendant attribution; the new behavior records a diagnostic explaining the payload root is gone and descendant list is unsafe to signal, then clears `active_launch`. A new test `missing_payload_is_diagnostic_not_a_sticky_launch_gate` covers this. Other touched launcher files appear to be rustfmt-only.
  invariants: Do not signal processes when the root payload identity is missing and only persisted descendants remain. Do not hide genuine still-running same-pid/different-start-identity conflicts. Do not weaken cooperative observed cleanup when the root payload exists. Do not change Capsule selection, external previous/current rotation, seed fallback, or runtime failure fallback semantics. Do not stage unrelated dirty files in `.codex`, `.morpheus`, `.playwright-cli`, or Android local config.
  forbidden_paths: no broad cleanup by pid list without live root attribution; no swallowing diagnostics silently; no deleting control/failure evidence; no App Bundle mutation; no release build/restart by owner; no formatting churn beyond the already-touched launcher files unless required by `cargo fmt`.
  expected_implementation_outline: Inspect the existing dirty launcher diff, separate behavioral change from rustfmt-only noise, and keep the behavioral change narrow in `reconcile_active_launch`. Ensure failure evidence remains typed/bounded through the existing `FailureProjection` path. Run rustfmt/checks if needed, add or preserve a focused test for the missing-root case, and commit only the four launcher files if validation passes.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Runtime-launcher tests covering missing payload root diagnostic; same-pid/different-start diagnostic still blocks as before; direct cleanup with live root still cleans or blocks safely; fallback/selection tests still pass; `cargo test --manifest-path codex-rs/Cargo.toml -p runtime-launcher`; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: not applicable after install; payload Runtime Capsule release id is unchanged (`sha256:64a000b0b7350e6fff65d655a53e60e7e9401fd3a199519d435883bf9efb9e55`) because the modified code is the outer `MorpheusLauncher` binary, not payload content.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM recorded user-provided dirty launcher changes and dispatched to fixed main owner for validation/review/commit. Owner delivered main commit `8d6ca36b3a78d0552654a706f6f18f4e2d2bb9a9` touching only `codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/supervisor.rs`; removed unrelated rustfmt-only launcher churn; reviewer approved after confirming missing-root no longer signals stale descendants and diagnostic write succeeds before clearing gate. Owner validation passed: `cargo test --manifest-path codex-rs/Cargo.toml -p runtime-launcher` -> 51+2 passed with existing dead_code warnings; `git diff --check`, `git diff --check HEAD~1 HEAD`, and `git show --check --stat HEAD` passed. PM inspected commit and accepted design alignment. PM temporarily stashed unrelated dirty state, built full mac package from clean canonical main `8d6ca36b3a78d0552654a706f6f18f4e2d2bb9a9` via `pnpm package:root-worker-prototype:mac`, then restored dirty state. Package succeeded with existing Vite/app-server/runtime-launcher warnings and produced `apps/root-worker-prototype/dist-app/Root Worker Prototype-arm64.dmg` plus `apps/root-worker-prototype/dist-app/Root Worker Prototype-darwin-arm64/Root Worker Prototype.app`. PM replaced `/Applications/Root Worker Prototype.app` with the newly built app bundle, keeping backup `/Applications/Root Worker Prototype.app.backup-20260918-143951`. Disk Launcher sha256 now matches the built app (`70c6b2a00c40e7f827dd83db55d063d220c8b64a943d8e995ac323b75b5a9b45`) and `codesign --verify --deep --strict --verbose=2 /Applications/Root Worker Prototype.app` passed. Running Launcher pid `83553` started at `Fri Sep 18 14:40:16 2026` from `/Applications/Root Worker Prototype.app/Contents/MacOS/MorpheusLauncher`; control state selected/active runtime remains `64a000...` with payload pid `83677`, active phase `running`. Self-debug attached to current Electron CDP `127.0.0.1:9223`: renderer URL points at `64a000...` payload as expected and console has 0 errors / 0 warnings.

- id: cross-project-user-message-submit-display
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/cross-project-user-message-submit-display
  task_type: bugfix/frontend-cross-project-message-submit-display-latency
  depends_on: user report on 2026-09-18 CST that `/telebot` sends do not display user messages and respond slowly; canonical main `900ba505942591178db21db4c0a52560a6dd3088`; installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:0c77916ab905541ddbfcf1d02ec89cbd4fe3760c1603a6a740e7041c1c395740`.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/App.tsx`, thread selection/submit/read helpers/tests, conversation display tests, possibly preload IPC only if submit target id comes from stale persisted selected state.
  base_commit: 900ba505942591178db21db4c0a52560a6dd3088
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Fix cross-project `/telebot` message send UX so the user’s submitted message appears immediately in the selected conversation and the app does not wait on slow full read/stale selection before showing it.
  design_intent: Sending a message is a typed current-thread event. The user’s own message should be visible optimistically/locally as soon as submit is accepted for the selected thread, independent of slow model response or history read. Cross-project selection state must consistently target the displayed thread, not a stale previous project thread id.
  current_problem_model: Installed self-debug after user report: `/telebot` project is active and UI shows `/telebot` Running/Thinking, but recent visible articles show assistant/tool output without a corresponding `You` message for the latest send. A renderer eval after selecting `/telebot` found DOM title `/telebot`, while `localStorage.morpheus.rootWorker.selectedThreadId` returned `/self` root thread id `01a08b3c...`; `window.codexDesktop.readThread(id, true)` then read that stale `/self` id and returned 0 turns. Runtime `list_agents('/telebot')` confirms `/telebot` is active. This suggests selected-thread identity, submit target, or optimistic display may be inconsistent across project switches, localStorage, and current React state.
  invariants: Do not change telebot business repo/code. Do not solve by forcing full `thread/read` before/after every send. Do not break subscribed-thread switch no-read semantics. User messages sent to a selected thread must appear in that thread display promptly and must not be attributed to a stale project/thread. Slow assistant/model/tool response must not block showing the submitted user message. Cross-project project selection and stored selection should remain coherent.
  forbidden_paths: no CSS-only placeholder; no path-specific `/telebot` special case; no global polling; no full-read workaround; no suppressing slow state; no changing compact architecture; no broad thread tree rewrite.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with focused App/thread tests simulating selected thread switch from one project to another, stale persisted selected thread id, and send on the displayed project root. Inspect the message submit handler’s source of selected thread id, optimistic local append path, read/subscribe request sequencing, and stale read response guards. Ensure submit targets the current selected/display thread from React state, not stale localStorage or delayed read state, and appends/merges the user message into the visible thread immediately. If the backend sends user item later, merge without duplicates.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Sending in `/telebot`/cross-project selected thread displays user message immediately even if assistant response is slow; stale stored selectedThreadId from previous project cannot redirect read/submit/display; user message persists after later thread/read snapshot; no duplicate user message when backend snapshot arrives; switching back to subscribed fresh thread still avoids full read; idle-unsubscribed switch still reads; console no errors; focused tests plus `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered in Runtime Capsule artifact `sha256:aa8903a892b542c3d2830f6ec10d6db8151ab4cc501114ff85adc2b64f7fa240`.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM self-debug confirmed installed UI can select `/telebot`, runtime `/telebot` is active, latest visible items lack the user message user reported, and renderer persisted selected thread id may be stale `/self` while DOM displays `/telebot`. PM opened dev-3 branch `fix/cross-project-user-message-submit-display` from canonical main `900ba505`. User clarified the message did reach `/telebot` because the thread started working and showed other messages; root issue is display/merge losing the submitted user message, not wrong target. Owner delivered `8fc73ff4f3e2df49de23cf681757c2e524b6da75`: `turn/started` now preserves userMessage items via narrow merge, later agent/tool output merges after it, and status notifications can reopen completed lifecycle to active via notification-specific helper while strong terminal statuses remain protected. PM merged to canonical main as `4e450a03b8bc6d11c101b0a5ab62780d5118b783`; main validation passed: `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/thread.test.ts src/lib/sendMessageFlow.test.ts src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts src/components/RightPanel.test.tsx` -> 315 pass; `git diff --check HEAD~1..HEAD` pass. PM built full mac Runtime Capsule `sha256:aa8903a892b542c3d2830f6ec10d6db8151ab4cc501114ff85adc2b64f7fa240`; restart request `call_L4yqHNxqxOJks4iHxuHZAEk8` completed via expected restart recovery. Installed self-debug confirmed renderer URL under `aa8903...`, console 0 errors/warnings, `/self` project aggregate shows active status, `/telebot` project aggregate shows `doing` with active agent, and frontend `listThreads()` returns `/self/owner_dev_2` active while its reviewer is completed.

- id: thread-subscription-idle-timeout-10m
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: chore/thread-subscription-idle-timeout-10m
  task_type: config/frontend-thread-subscription-idle-timeout
  depends_on: user request on 2026-09-18 CST to make unsubscribe timeout larger, around 10 minutes; canonical main `1fb22a540295c762554c9e19e99416fd5bcef8f1`.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/App.tsx`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts`, or related thread subscription idle timer helpers/tests.
  base_commit: 1fb22a540295c762554c9e19e99416fd5bcef8f1
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Increase conversation thread idle unsubscribe timeout to 10 minutes so recently viewed subscribed threads remain live longer before being unsubscribed.
  design_intent: Subscribed threads are the freshness contract for avoiding full read on switch. A short idle unsubscribe window causes too much churn and can make switching feel stale/laggy. Ten minutes is a better default balance between resource use and UX.
  current_problem_model: Recent selection-policy fix made loaded+subscribed threads skip full `thread/read`. That makes the idle unsubscribe threshold more product-visible: once a thread is auto-unsubscribed, switching back correctly performs a read+subscribe. User wants this threshold larger, around 10 minutes.
  invariants: Do not disable idle unsubscribe entirely. Do not change status broadcast semantics. Do not change selection policy except expected timeout-related tests. Missing/unloaded/unsubscribed threads must still read+subscribe. Loaded+subscribed fresh threads must still skip full read.
  forbidden_paths: no polling; no path-specific timeout; no full-read workaround; no changing backend thread status or compact semantics; no Runtime Capsule build/restart by owner.
  expected_implementation_outline: Locate the frontend idle unsubscribe timeout constant/config used when a selected/subscribed thread becomes unviewed. Change it to 10 minutes (`10 * 60 * 1000` or equivalent named constant). Prefer a named constant and update tests to assert 10-minute scheduling or policy behavior if existing tests cover the old value.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Focused tests confirm idle-unsubscribe scheduling uses 10 minutes; fresh subscribed switch still does not read; idle-unsubscribed switch still reads; related thread selection/thread tests pass; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered in Runtime Capsule artifact `sha256:aa8903a892b542c3d2830f6ec10d6db8151ab4cc501114ff85adc2b64f7fa240`.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM opened dev-3 branch `chore/thread-subscription-idle-timeout-10m` from canonical main `1fb22a54`. Owner delivered `8ca54a60c5361efad53639d86cd05cbe6b91240c`: changed `THREAD_SUBSCRIPTION_IDLE_UNSUBSCRIBE_MS` from 5 minutes to 10 minutes and added focused source-level test asserting the named constant drives `setTimeout`. Reviewer approved. Owner validation passed: `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts` -> 15 pass; `git diff --check` pass. PM inspected diff and confirmed no selection policy/status/compact changes; PM merged to canonical main as `900ba505942591178db21db4c0a52560a6dd3088`; main validation passed: same 15 tests and `git diff --check HEAD~1..HEAD`. PM delivered via full mac Runtime Capsule `sha256:aa8903a892b542c3d2830f6ec10d6db8151ab4cc501114ff85adc2b64f7fa240`; restart request `call_L4yqHNxqxOJks4iHxuHZAEk8` completed via expected restart recovery; installed renderer identity and console smoke passed.

- id: compact-context-after-summary-only
  owner: /self/owner_dev_2; follow-up owner /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer; follow-up reviewer /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2; follow-up checkout /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/compact-context-after-summary; follow-up branch fix/compact-thread-history-tests
  task_type: bugfix/compact-model-context-and-display-boundary
  depends_on: user clarification on 2026-09-18 CST that after compact the context should be compact summary + re-injected init context + normal post-compact continuation, not prior full user messages or replacement-history details; canonical main `1fb22a540295c762554c9e19e99416fd5bcef8f1`.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/conversationCompact.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/conversation.test.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/components/Conversation.tsx`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/components/Conversation.test.tsx`, and backend/context compaction prompt assembly if model-visible replacement history is injected outside frontend display.
  base_commit: 1fb22a540295c762554c9e19e99416fd5bcef8f1
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Make compact semantics match the product contract: after compact, model-visible and ordinary conversation display should continue from compact summary + freshly injected init context + post-compact content only. Full prior user messages and replacement-history details should not be reintroduced as ordinary context or default UI detail.
  design_intent: Compaction is a boundary that replaces prior history with a summary. `replacementHistory` was useful as an implementation/debug artifact, but the user-facing and model-facing contract should not carry old full messages forward after the boundary. Keeping pre-compact user messages visible or model-visible defeats compaction and makes the transcript look like the previous turn survived in full.
  current_problem_model: Current frontend code explicitly has a `keeps same-turn user messages visible across compact display boundary` test and `conversationCompact.ts` preserves user-message cells in the compact turn. The user observed prior/previous-turn user messages still shown after compact. This suggests either compact and prior user messages are in the same display turn, or the preservation rule is too broad. Separately, if backend/model context assembly uses `replacementHistory` after compact, it may be feeding old full user messages back to the model instead of summary + init context.
  invariants: Compact summary remains available and visible as the compact marker/summary. Fresh init context must still be injected for subsequent turns. User messages that truly occur after the compact boundary must remain visible and model-visible. Tool/command/live monitor display after compact must continue working. Do not lose current-turn user input that was sent after compact. Do not re-expand archived/replacement history into the main conversation.
  forbidden_paths: no CSS-only hiding while old messages remain in model context; no deleting real persisted rollout/history as a migration; no replacing compact summary with full replacementHistory; no path-specific `/self` workaround; no broad removal of all user messages in a turn; no full thread read/reload workaround; no leaving expandable compact details that show the prior full conversation by default.
  expected_implementation_outline: First identify whether the bug is frontend display-only, model-visible context assembly, or both. Update the compact boundary logic so entries before the `contextCompaction` item are archived/hidden regardless of role, while entries after the compact item remain visible. If preserving same-turn user messages is still needed, scope it only to user entries after the compact marker, not before it. Remove or disable default replacement-history detail exposure for compact rows if it reintroduces pre-compact content. Inspect backend prompt/context assembly for `replacementHistory` use; ensure post-compact model input uses compact summary plus re-injected init context and post-boundary items only. Add regression tests that reproduce a prior-turn or pre-compact user message appearing before compact and assert it is not in visible entries/cells nor model-visible compact continuation, while a user message after compact remains.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Pre-compact user message in same turn is hidden/archived behind compact marker, not shown as ordinary `You`; previous-turn user messages are not shown after latest compact; compact row does not expose replacement-history details containing old full messages by default; post-compact user message remains visible; compact summary remains accessible as summary; model-visible context builder, if touched, excludes replacementHistory full messages and includes compact summary + init context + post-compact items; existing conversation compact tests updated intentionally; run focused frontend tests for conversation/compact and relevant backend context tests if touched; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM confirmed user’s intended contract and opened dev-2 branch `fix/compact-context-after-summary` from canonical main `1fb22a54`. Owner_dev_2 delivered commit `5b4bc03aa75e63e57e1e8907c5d907002e7c6b35` with reviewer approval: compact production uses summary-only checkpoint, fresh init/runtime context + retained suffix are post-compact items, reconstruction ignores legacy replacementHistory, typed display projection suppresses replacementHistory, reload compact window start counts summary + post-compact baseline, and frontend compact boundary hides pre-compact same-turn user messages while preserving post-compact user messages. PM merged cleanly to canonical main as `845dda875a297892198f1dbc6f171ccb6820d865` and design inspection accepted the core contract. `thread-history compact` then failed 3 old tests still expecting `ThreadItem::ContextCompaction.replacement_history = Some(...)`; PM dispatched follow-up to `/self/owner_dev_3`, owner delivered `735898a30c196ce888f0efa146eccee03052eed5` with reviewer approval, and PM merged it to canonical main as `b0623b23d0fbd82a4364a02c060fb4a74396f509c` after resolving the one semantic conflict in `thread-history/src/lifecycle.rs`. Default-parallel `thread-service compact` then repeated the same staged compact summary timeout; PM dispatched new follow-up to `/self/owner_dev_3`, owner delivered `4559f04362d9fa87f9b831b8482bd2f4f1a6ea05` with reviewer approval, isolating process-global `AUTO_COMPACT_TEST_HOOK` by session pointer key + turn id, and PM merged it to canonical main as `76921248489aa457bd93f7d5afa34c1494daacf9`. PM validation passed. PM temporarily stashed unrelated dirty worktree state to build from clean `76921248489aa457bd93f7d5afa34c1494daacf9`, built full mac Runtime Capsule/package `sha256:64a000b0b7350e6fff65d655a53e60e7e9401fd3a199519d435883bf9efb9e55`, restart request `call_vphLzBhSXhYPnL5flN2rDjJT` completed via expected restart recovery, and PM restored the pre-build dirty worktree state. Installed self-debug confirmed renderer URL under `64a000...`, runtime-launcher control selected/activeLaunch on `64a000...` with previous `aa8903...`, console 0 errors/warnings, and UI snapshot shows `/self` active/running from `/Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace`.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered in Runtime Capsule artifact `sha256:64a000b0b7350e6fff65d655a53e60e7e9401fd3a199519d435883bf9efb9e55`.
  pending_sync_from_main: dev-2 still has unrelated dirty worktree and an unusable fixed owner thread path; do not force-sync or reuse until cleaned/recovered. dev-3 fast-forwarded to main `76921248489aa457bd93f7d5afa34c1494daacf9` after Capsule delivery.
  next_action: none for compact delivery; optionally sync dev-3 to main when idle.
  blockers: none.

- id: subscribed-thread-switch-no-read
  owner: /self/owner_dev_2
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2
  branch: fix/subscribed-thread-switch-no-read
  task_type: perf/ux-thread-selection-subscription-semantics
  depends_on: user clarification on 2026-09-18 CST; canonical main `a447dbdd6feb97d4faa60888318b8d6052a349a8`; installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:18456db54d2b07072588a28e42f3d401e6c64ab67f47a5b50c758b132d0732aa`.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/App.tsx`, possibly selection/subscription idle-unload code and focused tests; avoid backend read/list changes unless frontend lacks required subscription/unsubscribe facts.
  base_commit: a447dbdd6feb97d4faa60888318b8d6052a349a8
  status: merged_validated_pending_capsule_delivery
  objective: Make thread switching use live subscription semantics: a loaded + subscribed thread should keep conversation content fresh through realtime updates and should not full-read on every selection change; if a thread has been idle/unviewed beyond the configured unsubscribe/unload threshold, unsubscribe/free it and perform `thread/read` only when it is selected again.
  design_intent: Subscription is the conversation freshness contract. The previous conservative “selection changed => readAndSubscribe” policy fixed stale background display but made every A->B->A switch pay full `thread/read(includeTurns=true)`, which causes visible lag and conflicts with the intended model. The correct product shape is realtime updates while subscribed, plus bounded resource reclamation via idle unsubscribe/unload.
  current_problem_model: `decideThreadSelectionAction()` currently returns `readAndSubscribe` whenever `selectionChanged` is true, even for loaded/subscribed threads. `App.tsx` then calls `loadThread(selectedThreadId)`, and preload defaults `readThread(threadId, includeTurns=true)`, so every switch performs a full history read. User clarified this is wrong: subscribed threads should update live; only after a configured no-view idle period should they unsubscribe, and then selection should read.
  invariants: Loaded+subscribed threads must remain live-updated while not selected. Switching between still-subscribed threads must not call full `readThread(includeTurns=true)`. Missing/unloaded/unsubscribed threads must still read and subscribe on selection. Background followup display must remain fixed: if a subscribed background thread receives new durable messages, selecting it shows them without manual refresh. Live command/tool rows must not disappear or be overwritten by stale snapshots. Repeated fast switches must ignore stale read responses. Idle unsubscribe must be bounded/configured and not churn active/running threads incorrectly.
  forbidden_paths: no blanket disabling of refresh for every selection state; no stale cache forever; no frontend fake rows; no path-specific exceptions for `/self` or `/telebot`; no global unbounded polling; no reverting runtime-first `thread/read`; no hiding loading indicators while still doing the full read.
  expected_implementation_outline: Update the frontend selection policy so `selectionChanged && isLoaded && isSubscribed && notExpired` returns `none`/cache-display rather than `readAndSubscribe`. Introduce or reuse explicit freshness/idle-unsubscribe evidence rather than using selection change as a proxy for staleness. If idle unsubscribe state is already represented, wire it into the policy; otherwise add the narrowest frontend-side timestamp/TTL state needed to unsubscribe or mark a thread unsubscribed after it has not been viewed for the configured threshold. Keep selection read behavior for missing, unloaded, unsubscribed, stale/expired, and live-cached-before-init states. Adjust `App.tsx` to avoid blocking UI on full reads for subscribed fresh threads, while preserving request id guards for reads that still happen.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Tests for switching to a loaded+subscribed fresh thread does not call/read; background subscribed update then selection shows new rows from live subscription without full read; thread unsubscribed/expired by idle threshold then selected does read+subscribe; missing/unloaded/unsubscribed threads still read; fast A/B switches with in-flight reads still ignore stale snapshots; live command rows survive switch; no duplicate rows after background update + selection. Run `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts src/lib/thread.test.ts src/lib/conversation.test.ts`, relevant `App`/component tests if touched, and `git diff --check`. Fixed reviewer approval required.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required to install the thread switching performance/semantics fix; current installed release remains `sha256:18456db54d2b07072588a28e42f3d401e6c64ab67f47a5b50c758b132d0732aa`; delivery batch includes later test-only status boundary merge `1d34ea4c`.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST PM confirmed current policy does full `thread/read(includeTurns=true)` on every selection change and user corrected intended semantics: subscribed threads update realtime; if not viewed for longer than the configured time, unsubscribe, then read on next switch. PM synced dev-2 from main `a447dbdd` and dispatched to owner_dev_2. User then clarified all thread statuses realtime update is a related but separate task, so PM instructed owner to keep status out of this implementation while PM does read-only status subscription confirmation. Owner delivered `5a40c9a23246414a89eb46db1a6985c54a9b8c73`: loaded+subscribed+local selection returns `none`; missing/unloaded/unsubscribed/idle-unsubscribed still read+subscribe; tests added for fresh subscribed switch, live cache switch, and idle-unsubscribed switch. Reviewer approved. Owner validation: threadSelectionPolicy/thread/conversation tests 320 pass; `git diff --check` pass. PM merged to canonical main as `fa09ce54bf8693c06f8307bea8d5f0324933dacd`; main validation passed: `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/threadSelectionPolicy.test.ts src/lib/thread.test.ts src/lib/conversation.test.ts` -> 320 pass with existing Node `DEP0205`; `git diff --check HEAD~1..HEAD` pass.

  owner: /self/owner_dev_2

  objective: Separately confirm and, if needed, fix whether all thread statuses can update realtime without requiring each conversation thread to be full-turn subscribed/selected.
  design_intent: Conversation content freshness and thread lifecycle/status freshness are separate contracts. Conversation updates can depend on per-thread subscription and idle unsubscribe policy; status/list updates may need a lighter global/list-level lifecycle stream so every listed thread status stays current without forcing full history subscription or selection reads for every thread.
  current_problem_model: User expects every thread status to update realtime even when thread messages are not conversation-subscribed. PM read-only confirmation found backend `ThreadWatchManager` emits `ServerNotification::ThreadStatusChanged` through `OutgoingMessageSender::send_server_notification`, which broadcasts when no target connections are specified; transport delivers broadcasts to all initialized connections unless opted out. Frontend `App.tsx` handles global `thread/status/changed` notifications and updates local lifecycle status. Conversation/live message events are separate: they use `ThreadScopedOutgoingMessageSender` with `subscribed_connection_ids`, so message/content realtime and status realtime are not meant to be bound together.
  invariants: Do not solve status freshness by full-reading every thread. Do not keep every conversation fully subscribed solely for status. If current status signals are subscribe-scoped, prefer adding/reusing a lighter list/status subscription or lifecycle stream rather than conflating it with conversation subscription.
  forbidden_paths: no unbounded polling; no path-specific status hacks; no forcing all threads to `readThread(includeTurns=true)`; no mixing this into `subscribed-thread-switch-no-read`.
  last_update: 2026-09-18 CST queued as separate investigation after user clarified the concerns should be split. PM read-only confirmed status notifications are broadcast/global rather than conversation-subscription-scoped, while conversation live events are scoped to subscribed connection ids.   base_commit: 9549e166ade8cf3aef6675eb2d3dfec72e087b94
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Fix installed Browser panel navigation so `showBrowserView` + `navigateBrowserView('https://example.com/')` settles and the embedded page appears as an Electron BrowserView/web target instead of timing out after 15s.
  design_intent: Browser panel is an in-app native BrowserView surface. IPC navigation must be bounded and converge to a real loaded/error state based on BrowserView/WebContents lifecycle. A timeout in installed Capsule means the earlier Browser panel follow-ups are not installed-effective even though their unit tests pass. The fix must address the Electron BrowserView lifecycle/attachment/navigation path, not mask the error in React.
  current_problem_model: Installed self-debug on artifact `2c290678...` after full restart: renderer loaded cleanly with console 0 errors. Browser panel selected, `showBrowserView(bounds)` returned, but `navigateBrowserView('https://example.com/')` did not settle within a 10s Promise.race and UI later displayed `Browser navigation timed out after 15s`. Snapshot showed tab title `example.com`, URL `https://example.com/`, status timeout, no separate web target in `playwright-cli tab-list`. Host network is fine: `curl -I --max-time 10 https://example.com/` returned HTTP/2 200. This points to BrowserView/WebContents load/attach/visibility/state transaction not completing in packaged runtime, not external network failure.
  invariants: Do not use external browser. Do not fake loaded state or clear real errors. Do not remove bounded navigation timeout. Do not special-case example.com. Preserve bounds/raise/hide semantics and preload sandbox self-contained helper. BrowserView should not overlay conversation outside the measured right panel bounds.
  forbidden_paths: no UI-only suppression of `Browser navigation timed out`; no blanket treating `ERR_FAILED` or timeout as success; no hardcoded URL/domain; no opening in system browser; no disabling BrowserView detach/hide cleanup; no reverting runtime-first thread/read changes.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce in dev or packaged-like Electron with direct preload API sequence: show view with explicit bounds, navigate to `https://example.com/`, wait for lifecycle, verify webContents URL/title and BrowserView target visibility. Inspect `main.cjs` BrowserView manager navigation transaction: `loadURL`, pending navigation sequence, did-finish-load/did-fail-load/did-stop-loading, timeout cleanup, BrowserView attach/raise, and whether packaged sandbox/partition/session prevents network load. The fix should make navigation settle from real webContents events or a correctly classified bounded failure; if the page actually loads after timeout, state convergence must recognize the finished URL/title without leaving timeout error. Add focused Electron tests for the installed-failing sequence.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Installed/dev smoke for example.com or another stable HTTPS page loads/settles; navigation timeout still reports genuine unreachable URL; `showBrowserView` before navigate and setBounds after navigate do not break target; Browser panel bounds remain in right panel; existing Browser/preload tests and RightPanel tests pass; `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/components/RightPanel.test.tsx`; `node --test apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/browserViewManager.test.cjs apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/preloadSubscriptions.test.cjs`; root-worker build if frontend touched; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.   reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2
  branch: fix/active-command-timeline-placement-dev2

  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/conversation.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/conversation.test.ts`, possibly `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/thread.ts` only if active command current-state merge needs metadata, not right panel Terminal behavior.
  base_commit: f8710f681bec8437f9388c6ec6ae5463f40b63a9
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Keep live/running command items visible and updating, but render them in their original conversation/turn timeline position instead of pinning/append-floating them at the bottom of the current thread after newer messages.
  design_intent: Active command current state is useful as live evidence and for Terminal focus, but conversation chronology should remain stable. A long-running command that started at 17:41 must not move below later user/assistant/tool messages at 17:44 just because it is still running. The right panel Live Commands can remain a dedicated current-state surface; the conversation timeline should place the live command anchor by its owning turn/start timestamp when that context is known.
  current_problem_model: Installed self-debug of `/self` showed a running package command started at 17:41 rendered after later 17:43/17:44 messages, as the bottom-most command timeline row. DOM evidence: `.command-timeline-row` for `/bin/zsh -lc 'pnpm package:root-worker-prototype:mac'` appears after newer tool groups and user/assistant messages, while runtime `list_commands` only has that real running command. This suggests `buildConversationEntries()` or active command projection appends `thread.activeCommandItems` as a separate live bucket instead of merging them into the owning turn/time-ordered entry list.
  invariants: Do not hide running commands. Do not remove `activeCommandItems` from thread state because Terminal/RightPanel live command focus depends on it. Do not break completed command transcript rendering or compact/replay behavior. Do not duplicate the same command in both original turn and active bucket. Preserve current behavior that active commands without any owning turn can still be surfaced, but choose a deterministic fallback that does not destabilize existing timeline entries.
  forbidden_paths: no CSS sticky/display-only hack; no blanket suppression of active commands; no special-case for `pnpm package` or `/self`; no moving all commands to a separate panel only; no backend/runtime command-state mutation unless frontend projection alone cannot represent chronology.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with a focused conversation projection test: a thread has an older running command active item plus newer user/assistant/tool entries; `buildConversationEntries()` should put the live command at the older command's start/turn position, not after newer entries. Inspect how active command entries are currently synthesized and keyed/reused. Prefer merging active command current-state items into an existing matching turn item or sorting synthesized active entries by item `startedAtMs`/turn timestamp before final conversation ordering. If no timestamp/turn exists, preserve existing visible fallback but do not perturb entries with known timestamps. Ensure command updates with same id reuse entry identity and still show running state/output.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Tests for active command older than later messages stays before later user/assistant entries; active command with matching persisted/turn item updates that item rather than duplicating; active command with no timestamp/turn remains visible; completed command removal still removes active current-state row; existing conversation/thread tests pass; `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/conversation.test.ts src/lib/thread.test.ts`; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.
