  base_commit: c6ab29e38
  status: installed_effective
  objective: When switching away from and back to a live/current thread, command/tool items that were visible from live local notifications must not disappear just because `thread/read(includeTurns=true)` lacks persisted command items.
  design_intent: User clarified the intended contract: `thread/read` and current UI display should read a runtime-owned thread projection, not a request/UI path that directly treats persisted rollout as the authoritative display snapshot. Persisted rollout/history is still a durable restore/audit source, but it should be consumed by runtime when a thread is not loaded, then exposed through the same runtime projection contract. This does not mean every thread must remain resident forever: backend/runtime may persist idle threads and free in-memory state; on later read, runtime should detect the unloaded thread and read-through/restore from rollout into a consistent projection. Memory reclamation is a runtime lifecycle/cache policy, not a reason to split UI read semantics into a separate rollout-direct path. Active/live/waiting current command/tool state must come from runtime current projection and must not be overwritten by a stale persisted snapshot.
  current_problem_model: Installed self-debug after user report showed selected UI was Chat; after clicking `/self`, `.command-timeline-row` count was 0 and Thread Analysis showed `Live Commands / No live commands`. `window.codexDesktop.readThread('/self', true)` returned 3 turns / 76 items with counts `{contextCompaction:1,userMessage:4,agentMessage:70,collabAgentToolCall:3}` and no command typed items. Earlier in the same turn, before switching/hydration, DOM had multiple command timeline rows from live tool calls. This suggests local live command items are not merged/replayed when a live thread is selected/read, or metadata-only/live active command state is dropped by snapshot replacement.
  invariants: Do not duplicate command rows after repeated switch/read/subscribe cycles. Do not keep completed commands active after explicit command completion/removal. Do not hide command items or move them only to RightPanel Terminal. Preserve chronological placement from `active-command-item-pinned-bottom`. Preserve compact/replacement-history behavior and existing pending thread update replay semantics. Preserve the ability to unload/free idle threads for memory savings, as long as later reads restore through runtime rather than bypassing it.
  forbidden_paths: no CSS/display-only hack; no thread/path-specific hack for `/self`; no blanket retaining every stale command forever; no backend history rewrite; no disabling `readAndSubscribe` on selection; no reintroducing bottom-pinned active tail; no frontend cache-preservation or `mergeThreadSnapshot` retention as the primary fix; no persisted-rollout snapshot plus live-data complex merge patch as the primary design.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce in tests using a thread with live command/tool item notifications and a selected-thread read after switching. First locate why `readThread(includeTurns=true)` / selection hydration can bypass runtime projection and return a persisted rollout snapshot without command/tool typed items. Prefer moving/fixing the contract at the app-server/runtime read boundary: loaded/active/waiting threads return runtime current projection; unloaded/history/terminal threads may be restored/read-through from rollout inside runtime and then returned as the same projection type. Rollout reads should remain runtime-internal restore/durable-history inputs, not a UI/read authoritative alternate source. Frontend tests can prove switching no longer drops rows, but the main fix should not be frontend-only merging of stale and live data.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Tests for visible live command/tool rows surviving selected-thread switch/read; repeated read does not duplicate rows; explicit command completion/removal clears active current-state; terminal/final unloaded thread can still be read from durable history through runtime without retaining stale active commands; idle/unloaded read-through-from-rollout path does not require permanent in-memory residency; existing conversation/thread tests pass; backend read/listing/history tests if app-server/runtime touched; `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype test -- src/lib/conversation.test.ts src/lib/thread.test.ts`; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required. PM will merge into current pending capsule batch before packaging/restart.

User then clarified architecture: live/current display should not read authoritative snapshots directly from persisted rollout, and likely all `thread/read` should go through runtime projection; runtime itself may load from rollout when a thread is unloaded, and may persist+free idle threads to save memory, but that is a lifecycle/cache policy under runtime rather than a separate UI/read rollout path.   objective: Fix the client/thread list lifecycle projection so a long-lived fixed owner agent that was previously completed but is reused for new work is shown as active/working while it is actually active, not stale completed.
  design_intent: Fixed owner threads are reused across tasks. A completed lifecycle is terminal for one completed turn/session phase, but it must not permanently freeze the UI/list snapshot against later authoritative active/waiting updates for the same thread when the runtime reopens that owner. The client should reflect live runtime facts and current metadata while preserving real final/completed when no newer live activity exists.
  current_problem_model: During Browser follow-up, PM `read_agent('/self/owner_dev_3')` showed lifecycle active while the user observed the client showing dev-3 complete. After the owner later completed, installed client `window.codexDesktop.listThreads()` still returned `/self/owner_dev_3` as final/completed with stale `gitInfo.branch: fix/hide-live-command-tail` and old restart-recovery preview, whereas `read_agent` returned the current Browser follow-up final message. This suggests list/thread snapshot merging or projection can preserve a previous final/completed + stale metadata across a reused owner task, likely around frontend `mergeThreadLifecycleStatus` / `preserveFinalLifecycleStatus` or backend list/read source freshness. There is known code that blocks completed->active in authoritative merges unless `allowCompletedReopen` is set.
  invariants: Do not reopen genuinely completed inactive threads based on stale or non-authoritative data. Do not weaken strong terminal errored/interrupted/shutdown semantics. Do not special-case `/self/owner_dev_3` or any branch name. Preserve previous fixes where stale active must not override persisted completed with no live evidence. Preserve active command/subagent/subscription waiting semantics and project completion notifications.
  forbidden_paths: no UI-only label override; no blanket “active always wins” that regresses stale active cache bugs; no path/name/task-specific hacks; no deleting final preservation entirely; no fake thread metadata; no hiding completed rows.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with a focused unit scenario: existing frontend thread snapshot is final/completed with old metadata, then an authoritative thread/status/list/read update for the same id indicates active/running or waiting plus newer metadata; merged state must reopen and update metadata. Decide whether the fix belongs in frontend merge options (`allowCompletedReopen` for authoritative thread/status notifications with live evidence), backend list/read projection freshness, or both. Ensure non-authoritative partial snapshots still preserve completed against stale active. Add tests for completed->active reopen, completed->waiting reopen, strong terminal not reopened, stale/non-authoritative active not reopening completed, and metadata/preview/gitInfo updating when authoritative reopened snapshot arrives.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Frontend unit tests around `mergeThreadLifecycleStatus`, `mergeThreadSnapshot`, and notification/list refresh handling; if backend touched, focused Rust tests for list/read live overlay of reused subagent. Verify with current installed/repro evidence where possible using self-debug: listThreads/readThread for `/self/owner_dev_3` should align with runtime after a reused owner receives new work. Run focused frontend tests, any touched backend tests/builds, `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype build` if frontend logic touched, and `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required.

- id: computer-use-background-observe-and-ax-input
  owner: /self/owner_dev_2
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer

  branch: feature/computer-use-background-observe-ax-input

  depends_on: user approved on 2026-09-17 CST the proposed Computer Use background app capability model; canonical main `1aa855741951c81e2267e9515ee90711fde73a35`; installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:22f8360a915a5c7440cb01c86882dd4c534ad7b2a7329709fa0708389b95917d`.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseMacNative.swift`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, tests, README/skill docs.
  base_commit: 1aa855741951c81e2267e9515ee90711fde73a35
  status: merged_pending_capsule_delivery
  objective: Add safe Computer Use support for background apps: background window/screen observation and text/element discovery where platform evidence is available, plus background text input only through explicit Accessibility writable-element semantics; keep generic keyboard/mouse side effects foreground-gated.
  design_intent: Support useful background work without weakening Computer Use safety. Background read operations should gather real target-window evidence without stealing focus when macOS allows it. Background write operations must be semantic and target-scoped, not blind global input: only AX writable text/value fields may be changed while the app remains background, with audit evidence. Generic click/key/hotkey/type/drag/scroll remain foreground-required and may activate/reobserve as today.
  current_problem_model: Current Computer Use records background targets as metadata-only for observe and returns a limitation that target-window crop and AX extraction require frontmost. Side effects activate background targets before native input. The user now wants background app operation such as input and screenshot. The safe product boundary is: background observe/findText can be added; background input is allowed only when a unique AX writable target is identified and set by Accessibility; background coordinate/key/mouse operations remain unsupported/foreground-required.
  invariants: Do not fake background screenshots or elements. Do not send CGEvent keyboard/mouse input to a non-frontmost app. Preserve target identity gates, risk policy, confirmation/audit evidence, trace cleanup, overlay semantics, and existing foreground Computer Use behavior. Any background write must be tied to a concrete target app/window/AX element identity and return typed evidence including method, target visibility, matched element, before/after or requested value status, and unsupported/permission failure reasons. Screenshot data remains bounded/omittable.
  forbidden_paths: no blanket background typing via pasteboard/Cmd+V; no background coordinate clicks/drags/scrolls/hotkeys; no app-specific hardcoded selectors; no hidden foreground activation for APIs labelled background-only without reporting it; no lowering risk gates for sensitive text; no claiming success when AX set-value is unavailable or unverifiable; no UI mirror/simulation replacing real app state.
  expected_implementation_outline: Introduce explicit background-capable action/options naming, e.g. `observe --background` / `findText --background` and a semantic input action such as `setText`/`typeText` with `mode: background-ax` or `--background-text <query>=<text>`; owner may choose exact CLI/API shape but it must be explicit and documented. Extend the native macOS observe path to collect target-window bounds/crop metadata/screenshot and AX candidates for background target windows when possible, using typed limitations for minimized/protected/inaccessible windows. Add a native AX set-value path that only operates on a unique writable text/value candidate, without making the app frontmost; otherwise return `foreground-required`/`unsupported`. Keep existing `type` side-effect path foreground-gated and activation-based. Update CLI result modes such as `background-observe`, `background-ax-read`, `background-ax-write`, `foreground-required`, `unsupported`.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Unit tests for background observe returning real crop/AX when native evidence provides it and typed limitations when unavailable; `findText --background` finds unique/multiple/no matches without side effects; background AX text write succeeds only on unique writable element and records evidence; background write blocks on multiple/no/non-writable/truncated/AX-permission-failed candidates; generic `type`/key/click/hotkey/scroll/drag still foreground-gate/activate as before; high-risk text still requires confirmation; output omission strips background screenshot data; REPL and batch action attribution remains correct; cleanup still runs on failures. Run focused Computer Use tests, Swift parse, CLI tests, docs/skill update checks, and `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required. PM will merge and build/restart because this changes installed native/runtime Computer Use behavior.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required for merge `e9d49763e20e6bb93eedc879cbab4c62cd53bcd7`; defer until Browser follow-up because current installed Browser smoke is failing and the next capsule should include both fixes.
  last_update: 2026-09-17 CST PM used `computer-use-cli` skill and dispatched to owner_dev_2 on clean checkout at `1aa855741951c81e2267e9515ee90711fde73a35`; Browser fix remains in parallel on dev-3 with no intended file overlap. Reviewer `/self/owner_dev_2/reviewer` found a blocker during review: background target with `windowId` directly calls `screenshotCapture(root, { windowId })`, and crop capture failure currently fails the entire observe instead of returning available background observe/AX evidence plus typed limitation. Owner fixed the reviewer blocker and delivered commit `650531e32cc2ebfdffe34084eba8767f52185ead`: background observe now supports real target window bounds/window-id crop/AX elements and degrades crop failures to typed limitations; `setText` uses JS pre-match plus Swift native re-match to write only a unique writable AX value element; ordinary keyboard/mouse side effects remain activate + reobserve + frontmost gated; CLI/REPL gained `setText` / `--set-text query=value`; README and local `computer-use-cli` skill docs updated. Fixed reviewer approved after three rounds with no blockers. Owner validation passed: `node --test apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.test.cjs` 41 pass; `node --test scripts/morpheus-computer-use.test.mjs` 31 pass; Swift parse pass; `git diff --check` pass. PM inspected diff against design and confirmed no background CGEvent keyboard/mouse, no app-specific selector, no foreground-window fallback for background target, and no fake screenshot/success. PM dev-2 validation passed with the same 41 + 31 tests, Swift parse, and diff check. PM merged to canonical main as `e9d49763e20e6bb93eedc879cbab4c62cd53bcd7`; main validation passed with the same 41 + 31 tests, Swift parse, and `git diff --check HEAD^..HEAD`.

  base_commit: 1aa855741951c81e2267e9515ee90711fde73a35
  status: merged_pending_capsule_delivery
  objective: Fix the installed built-in Browser panel so normal URLs such as `https://example.com` load and display in the embedded Browser surface, state settles consistently, and stale/incorrect errors do not remain after successful navigation.
  design_intent: The Browser panel is a real embedded web surface inside the ChatGPT GUI/Morpheus app. The fix must make Electron BrowserView/WebContentsView lifecycle, preload state subscription, React local error state, and displayed tab state converge on the real active tab state. Successful final navigation should clear earlier transient UI/local errors and leave loading false; real failures should remain visible and bounded.
  current_problem_model: Installed self-debug reproduced that `window.codexDesktop.navigateBrowserView('https://example.com')` returns a BrowserPanelState with `url: https://example.com/`, `title: Example Domain`, `error: null`, but `loading: true`; the UI then shows title/address for Example Domain while also showing `Error invoking remote method 'codex:browser:navigate': Error: ERR_FAILED (-2) loading 'https://example.com/'`. `playwright-cli --s=default tab-list` only shows the main Root Worker renderer, no BrowserView target. Console now shows 8 repeated errors: `Uncaught TypeError: listener is not a function` at preload `onBrowserState` around line 151, indicating the browser state subscription bridge can be called with a non-function or stale listener and can throw on every pushed state.
  invariants: Do not launch an external browser for in-app navigation. Preserve the open-external button, URL normalization/security policy, tab model, back/forward/reload/stop semantics, and native overlay hide/show/bounds behavior. Do not fake Browser content in React or display-only rows. Keep Browser state typed and reload-safe.
  forbidden_paths: no UI-only hiding of `ERR_FAILED`; no blanket swallowing of all `did-fail-load` errors; no unbounded retry/reload loop; no special-casing `example.com`, SIA, or a single URL; no changing self-debug/Playwright to use external Chrome; no broad redesign of RightPanel.
  expected_implementation_outline: Start by tracing `loadBrowserPanelTabUrl`, `did-start-loading`, `did-stop-loading`, `did-fail-load`, title/location updates, attach/detach/bounds, and `subscribeBrowserState`. Determine whether `ERR_FAILED (-2)` is caused by a benign abort/replaced navigation, attach/load ordering, listener misuse, or stale localError. Fix the source lifecycle/state machine: successful final navigation must clear relevant local/tab errors and settle loading false; benign aborted/replaced loads may be ignored only with a narrow predicate and test; real failures must surface with loading false. Harden the preload subscription bridge so invalid listeners cannot poison future Browser state notifications, while preserving valid subscriptions.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Tests for successful Browser navigation state (url/title/error null/loading false); transient aborted/replaced prior navigation does not leave stale error if the final navigation succeeds; real main-frame failure surfaces bounded error and loading false; preload `subscribeBrowserState` ignores/rejects invalid listeners without throwing on later valid state pushes; show/hide/setBounds does not mark active tab failed; any touched React BrowserPanel path clears `localError` on successful state. Run focused Electron CJS tests, focused React tests if touched, `pnpm --filter @my-codex/root-worker-prototype build`, and `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required. PM will merge and build/restart because this is installed GUI behavior.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required after follow-up merge because current installed release `sha256:2248efdd9be4b9266097aac3914adae811f5612d46385a6f8531a9d645750a60` still fails Browser smoke.
  last_update: 2026-09-17 CST PM reproduced installed GUI issue with self-debug/frontend-debug/playwright-cli. Confirmed this is ChatGPT GUI/Morpheus Browser panel, not codex_cli. Dispatched to owner_dev_3 on clean checkout at `1aa855741951c81e2267e9515ee90711fde73a35`. Owner delivered `00d3f10bda8d29698e729e745f9d6e19e2d72b98`: added navigation sequence/finish-url evidence, narrow rejected-load success convergence, delayed in-flight `ERR_FAILED(-2)` handling, hardened preload Browser/Terminal state subscriptions against invalid listeners, and cleared React BrowserPanel stale `localError` on successful state while preserving real tab errors. Fixed reviewer `/self/owner_dev_3/reviewer` approved after three rounds; key blocker about real failures being mis-cleared was fixed by requiring same sequence + finishedUrl/currentUrl target match.   objective: Keep the left thread tree project ordering stable across refreshes, thread updates, lifecycle changes, and selection changes, while pinning `/self` as the default first project.
  design_intent: The left project tree is navigation structure, not an activity feed. Runtime/list refreshes may update row contents and child thread status, but must not shuffle top-level project groups in a way that breaks spatial memory. `/self` is the system workspace and should remain the predictable first entry when present.
  current_problem_model: The installed UI appears to reorder left-side project groups when thread/list data changes, likely due to sorting by activity/update time or relying on incoming list order from backend/runtime. User explicitly wants project order unchanged, with `/self` defaulted to top.
  invariants: Preserve existing project grouping, thread selection, unread/status/live update behavior, and thread ordering within each project unless the code currently has an explicit tested rule that must remain. Project row contents can update; the order of existing top-level projects should remain stable for the session/persisted source order. Newly discovered projects should append in a deterministic position after existing non-`/self` projects unless an existing source-order rule already defines placement. `/self` must be first when present, without duplicating or hiding it.
  forbidden_paths: no path-specific hacks beyond the semantic `/self` pin; no freezing the entire sidebar so status/children stop updating; no hiding projects; no relying on unstable object key iteration or backend incidental order; no broad visual redesign; no changing recovery/followup/runtime semantics.
  expected_implementation_outline: Find the single frontend/helper layer that derives top-level project groups for the left thread tree. Introduce/repair a stable project-order policy: project identity keys retain prior relative order across subsequent data refreshes; `/self` sorts before all others; new projects are inserted deterministically without reordering existing projects. Keep child thread/status updates flowing through existing typed data. Add focused tests at the pure helper/policy layer and, if practical, component/state tests that simulate list refresh with changed update times/order and verify project order remains unchanged.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Tests for initial order with `/self` first; backend/list order changes do not reorder existing projects; activity/update time changes do not reorder projects; newly discovered project insertion is deterministic and does not disturb existing order; `/self` appearing later moves/pins to top once without duplication; existing selected project/thread remains selected after refresh; no duplicate project groups. Run focused frontend tests for touched helpers/components plus `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval required. PM will merge and decide capsule delivery because this is installed UI behavior.

The client stayed on `/self`; `/telebot` sidebar row still rendered `todo ... 4 agents` with no visible unread/update state. After manually clicking `/telebot`, heading changed to `/telebot` but conversation still showed only the older 14:36/14:51 messages; the new test message/reply were absent. Console had 0 errors/warnings.

Background updates must not silently navigate the user away from active work unless there is an explicit product decision and test coverage.

The bug is duplicate visible representation of the same recovery fact, not that `/cp_http_api` received the fact.
  current_problem_model: User clarified the intended design: all non-complete threads should receive recovery. The latest installed `/cp_http_api` read shows raw persisted turns contain one userMessage for `runtime-restart:call_2Wr41xJGlTMo9wDdCEQHKcCi` in turn `01a0ae04-55c7-74e1-8e2c-cf26efa9925a` (`item-26`), yet UI displayed two recovery messages. Existing tests already mention presentation de-dupe across compact details (`conversationPresentation.test.ts`) and command audit counting (`conversation.test.ts`), suggesting the duplicate may come from current turn + compact/replacementHistory/details, a typed client recovery event plus durable user row, or another conversation projection path rendering the same recovery id twice.
  invariants: Preserve the design that every non-complete thread receives restart recovery. Do not narrow recipients to only `/self`. Do not delete persisted history. Do not hide all recovery prompts. The canonical visible key should be recovery id/marker plus item identity semantics, not fragile Chinese text matching alone. Existing status/lifecycle DB semantics must remain untouched. Independent different recovery ids (`call_VL4h...` vs `call_2Wr...`) must remain separate visible rows.
  forbidden_paths: no `/cp_http_api` special case; no recipient narrowing; no backend history rewriting; no broad filtering that hides distinct restart ids; no CSS/display-only hack without fixing projection data; no reintroducing lifecycle read/list inference.
  expected_implementation_outline: Start by reproducing the duplicate in installed/current data and then in focused frontend projection tests. Inspect `conversationPresentation`/`conversation` recovery de-dupe helpers and any typed client recovery event projection. Ensure compact `replacementHistory`, command audit output, and top-level userMessage do not each create separate visible recovery bubbles for the same `runtime-restart:<id>`. If the backend sends both a durable user message and a typed client recovery event for the same id into the same thread view, normalize them into one visible row while preserving audit details. Add/adjust tests around current turn plus compacted replacement history and active non-complete root recovery.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Frontend/unit tests proving same recovery id appears once when present in top-level turn plus compact replacement/details; distinct recovery ids both appear; non-recovery same-text user messages are preserved; typed client recovery plus durable recovery user row does not double-render if both represent same recovery id; all existing conversation recovery presentation tests pass; focused thread/conversation tests pass; `git diff --check`. PM will merge, build capsule, restart, and installed-verify `/cp_http_api` shows one visible row for a new recovery id.

  objective: Fix the lifecycle/status projection bug where a subagent with persisted completed turns and `task_complete` is still surfaced to the client as top-level `active`, and ensure completion/recovery notification semantics are not swallowed by stale active/watch status.
  design_intent: The canonical runtime fact is that completed agent lifecycle must win over stale watch/cache active overlays unless there is a real newer live turn or waiting runtime fact. UI/list/read/status notifications should converge with inter-agent `read_agent/list_agents` facts instead of carrying a stale active state forward.
  current_problem_model: `read_agent` and `list_agents` return `final/completed` for `/cp_http_api/owner_api/review_service_list_stage/concurrency_recheck`. Current installed client `window.codexDesktop.listThreads()` and `readThread(id, includeTurns=true)` return top-level `active`; with turns included, both turns are `completed`, last completedAt is `1789380146`, and rollout contains `task_complete`. Owner_main found `thread_status_changed_lifecycle_status` treats only errored/interrupted/shutdown as strong terminal, so `Completed` can be overwritten by stale watch `Active` and persisted to state DB; frontend authoritative merge also allows active to overwrite completed.
  invariants: Do not special-case this thread path. Do not rewrite rollout history. Do not hide active in UI text/CSS. Preserve legitimate rerun/in-progress semantics: a genuinely newer live turn, active command, child, subscription, or waiting runtime fact may make a previously completed thread active again. Preserve provider-neutral lifecycle/status facts and avoid tool-name/string hacks.
  forbidden_paths: no `/cp_http_api/...` or `concurrency_recheck` special cases; no broad “completed is always strong terminal forever” rule that blocks legitimate rerun/resume; no frontend-only cosmetic fix while app-server APIs still return active; no fake recovery message injection; no destructive reset or manual copying from dev to main.
  expected_implementation_outline: Start in app-server status merge: make authoritative `AgentStatus::Completed` produce final/completed when there is no real live in-progress/waiting fact and the competing status is only stale watch/cache active. Ensure `emit_thread_status_changed_notification_to_connections` does not persist stale active in that case, and `thread/list`/`thread/read` converge on completed for persisted terminal subagents. Add a narrow frontend guard only as defense if stale authoritative active notifications can still arrive without a live turn/generation. Then check parent-side completion/recovery notification path so waiting->final sends a status/completion update rather than being deduped as already active.
  minimum_regression_matrix: Rust unit for `thread_status_changed_lifecycle_status(Some(Completed), None, watch Active, has_in_progress_turn=false)` returning completed; Rust tests preserving `has_in_progress_turn=true` and real waiting command/child/subscription as active/waiting; app-server/list/read test for persisted native subagent `task_complete` plus stale active cache returning final/completed; frontend test if touched proving authoritative stale active does not reopen completed without live turn evidence; parent notification regression for child waiting->final if feasible; focused tests and `git diff --check`.

- id: computer-use-production-quality-program
  owner: PM coordination
  reviewer: n/a

  files: Computer Use manager/native bridge/CLI/docs/tests/UI safety surfaces across staged tasks.
  base_commit: eb2e53cb5d56c3a325a55b2005eb1d6074010bf4
  status: queued_program
  objective: Bring Morpheus Computer Use from a low-level desktop-control prototype to a reliable, ergonomic, auditable, agent-usable capability comparable in day-to-day usefulness to Codex/ChatGPT Computer Use.
  design_intent: Do this as staged product-quality tranches with real installed smoke after each risky native/runtime change. Avoid a single huge branch that mixes native input reliability, CLI UX, perception, long-lived sessions, and safety UI. Keep direct desktop semantics: actions happen in the real target app; Morpheus provides target selection, safety gates, observation, audit, and evidence.
  current_problem_model: Current strengths are target activation/reobserve/mismatch gate, agent cursor overlay, click/key/drag native paths, screenshot evidence, and installed CDP validation. Current gaps are reliable text typing, ergonomic command surface, richer action vocabulary, long-lived interactive session/repl/server, app/window screenshot semantics beyond foreground desktop capture, OCR/element perception, action result verification, and user-facing safety/interrupt UX.
  invariants: preserve target identity gate, no wrong-foreground side effects, `move` does not move system cursor, click/drag restore cursor when they temporarily use native mouse events, typed failures instead of fake success, complete trace/audit evidence, and no renderer/Playwright shortcut for real desktop actions.
  forbidden_paths: no one-off TextEdit-only product path; no hidden global clipboard pollution; no deleting safety gates to make demos pass; no app-mirror panel that changes where the real action happens; no broad unreviewed branch; no declaring installed effective without real installed smoke.
  expected_implementation_outline: Tranche 1 fixes native `type` and adds CLI shorthand. Tranche 2 adds action vocabulary and reliable evidence (`hotkey`, `scroll`, `doubleClick`, `rightClick`, pause/wait, screenshot/crop options). Tranche 3 adds a Morpheus Computer Use-specific long-lived `server`/`repl` session so agents can observe-think-act without giant JSON batches; it should borrow useful ergonomics from Codex/Node REPLs (help, history, scriptability, JSON/raw modes) but remain centered on real desktop target state, safety gates, evidence, and trace continuity rather than becoming a generic SDK/model REPL. Tranche 4 improves perception: foreground/window screenshot crop, OCR/accessibility element extraction where available, and selector-by-text/element helpers that compile to coordinates. Tranche 5 adds safety UX/productization: confirmation policies for risky actions, emergency stop/cancel/takeover, before/after diff evidence, and better docs/skill defaults.
  minimum_regression_matrix: each tranche must have unit/focused tests, real controlled app smoke, fixed reviewer approval, PM merge, Capsule build/restart when native/runtime behavior changes, and installed self-debug/Computer Use validation before `installed_effective`.
  pending_capsule_delivery: per tranche.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST PM created production-quality program after user requested "彻底做好".
  next_action: Tranche 4 is installed effective; dispatch Tranche 5 `computer-use-safety-ux-and-audit` now that perception/session core is stable.

  objective: Add a long-lived `server`/`repl` Computer Use session so agents can observe, reason, and continue actions without one giant JSON batch.
  design_intent: Match real agent workflow while preserving one trusted session state, approvals, overlay, cursor, screenshots, and trace continuity.   current_problem_model: Current `run` is a single-process batch; all dependent steps must be prewritten, making interactive correction and verification clumsy.

- id: computer-use-native-type-false-positive
  owner: /self/owner_dev_2
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2
  branch: fix/computer-use-native-type-textedit
  task_type: bugfix/computer-use-native-input
  depends_on: installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:bfae2d62fabed52153aa3d28ae3910e0d25de2b73b62650bfc6d8fe0f7d2a6c8` / sourceCommit `eb2e53cb5d56c3a325a55b2005eb1d6074010bf4`; user asked on 2026-09-16 CST to test Computer Use with more operations.
  files: likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseMacNative.swift`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.test.cjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.test.mjs`, README/skill text.
  base_commit: eb2e53cb5d56c3a325a55b2005eb1d6074010bf4
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Fix Computer Use `type` action false-positive behavior on real macOS targets and add a more ergonomic CLI wrapper so common actions/smokes do not require hand-written large JSON batches.
  design_intent: A completed Computer Use side-effect trace must mean the native action was delivered plausibly to the frontmost matched target, not merely that a CGEvent was posted without error. The `type` action should be reliable for ordinary editable controls such as TextEdit, while preserving the existing safety gate: background targets are activated, re-observed, and matched before sending input.
  current_problem_model: PM installed-smoked a controlled temp TextEdit file `/tmp/morpheus-cu-smoke.7V4YlN/smoke.txt`. Batch `start/observe/move/click/Cmd+A/type/Cmd+S/drag/Escape/observe/stop` completed all trace entries, but both disk file and `tell application "TextEdit" to get text of front document` stayed unchanged. A narrower batch `click/key a/type X/Cmd+S` proved background activation and click succeeded, and `key a` inserted/saved `a`, but `type X` still did not insert. Native Swift currently implements `typeText` via `CGEvent(keyboardEventSource:nil, virtualKey:0)` plus `keyboardSetUnicodeString` and always returns ok after posting events. Separately, the current CLI only exposes raw `--actions '<json-array>'`, which is acceptable for the runtime boundary but unnecessarily cumbersome for normal agent/human use and led to large fragile shell invocations during PM smoke.
  invariants: Keep target activation/re-observe/mismatch gates; do not deliver input to wrong foreground app; do not regress `key` actions, click/drag cursor restoration, move not moving system cursor, overlay target-bound behavior, TCC failures, or trace evidence. Do not use renderer/preload/Playwright or AppleScript as the product action path. Do not fake success for `type` if the chosen native injection cannot work.
  forbidden_paths: no deleting/weakening target gates; no replacing Computer Use with app-specific TextEdit AppleScript; no global pasteboard side effect without saving/restoring clipboard and documenting evidence; no uncontrolled typing into user apps during owner validation; no patching installed artifacts directly; no treating the PM smoke as sufficient if file/doc content verification still fails.
  expected_implementation_outline: First reproduce with a controlled temp TextEdit document. Investigate why Unicode `CGEvent` posts do not insert while `key a` does. Prefer a durable native macOS input method for text: either correct CGEvent Unicode delivery with proper source/timing/focus, or a bounded pasteboard-based text insertion path that saves/restores clipboard and posts Cmd+V only after target is frontmost/matched. Update native result/evidence so `type` reports method/characterCount and failures surface typed. Add tests around evidence and failure plumbing; real smoke should verify TextEdit document/file content changes. Also add a small ergonomic CLI layer on top of the existing `run --actions` primitive, e.g. `run --action key:cmd+s`, repeated `--action ...`, `--text ...`, `--click x,y`, `--move x,y`, `--drag x1,y1:x2,y2`, or a named `smoke-textedit`/`smoke --app TextEdit --text ...` helper. Keep the JSON batch path for full fidelity and tests; the wrapper should compile to the same normalized action array and print the equivalent actions in JSON output/evidence.
  minimum_regression_matrix: focused unit tests for type evidence/failure path and CLI shorthand parsing/compilation; existing Computer Use/CLI/overlay tests still pass; Swift parse; JS/CJS checks; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval. Owner smoke against a temp TextEdit `.txt` must prove `type` changes document/file content, `key a` still works, background activation evidence remains, and click/drag restore system cursor. Owner should demonstrate the same smoke using the new wrapper, not a hand-written long JSON command. PM will merge, rebuild/restart, and repeat installed TextEdit smoke before marking installed effective.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered via Runtime Capsule `sha256:26d4651a29e8799b75672f6109221451c1dfef49f7b7323d443c63e410141fcc` / sourceCommit `4b107da9d3668239c5521966aedd0827f0f6e63e` after restart request `call_cLtVVJD2Pb52KyGuePVUa5Bc`.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST PM discovered the bug during installed multi-action smoke: `key` inserted `a`, but `type` did not insert text despite completed trace. User also pointed out that the current raw JSON command is too complex and should be further wrapped; scope expanded to include a small CLI ergonomic wrapper over the same action session. Owner delivered `401eb25300afd9bef92e120bfeef1f6b9ec82d40` with fixed reviewer approval: native `type` now uses pasteboard + Cmd+V with pasteboard save/restore and evidence, Swift native errors are surfaced instead of fake success, and CLI shorthand flags compile to the same action array. PM reran focused tests/checks, Swift parse, diff check, and a real TextEdit shorthand smoke on dev-2; all passed. PM merged to canonical main as `4b107da9d3668239c5521966aedd0827f0f6e63e`; main focused tests/checks passed. PM built/signed Capsule `sha256:26d4651a...`, restarted via `call_cLtVVJD2Pb52KyGuePVUa5Bc`, confirmed selected/current/activeLaunch on the new release, and installed-smoked shorthand `--click --key a --type ... --key cmd+s --drag ... --key escape --observe --stop` against a temp TextEdit file. File content contains `a INSTALLED_TYPE_VERIFY_1789559169...`, trace shows target activation/reobserve frontmost, click/drag cursor restore, and type evidence `method:"pasteboard-cmd-v", pasteboardRestored:true`.
  next_action: none; task installed effective. Continue queued production-quality tranches starting with action vocabulary when checkout is synced.

  base_commit: 9025bc3b591d5bb071fe21a2155ffffd7a43048e
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Allow Computer Use CLI side-effect actions against a selected target app without requiring the user to manually make that target frontmost first.
  design_intent: User's `run --actions` request is the operation boundary and target app is the real operation surface. Removing the frontmost precondition must not mean sending click/type/key/drag to whatever app is currently foreground. Instead, the Computer Use session should bring or verify the requested target app/window into an actionable foreground/matched state itself, then execute native actions with traceable activation/focus evidence.
  current_problem_model: Current `policyForAction` blocks non-`move` actions when `targetVisibility !== "frontmost"` with `target-mismatch`. Installed smoke proved background `key`/`click` are blocked for this reason. This prevents agent use of a background app even when the user explicitly targeted it in the CLI command.
  invariants: `move` still must not move the macOS system cursor. Side effects must never be delivered to the wrong/current foreground app. Activation/focus attempts must be explicit, bounded, auditable, and fail typed if target cannot be made frontmost/matched. Keep native bridge execution, target/window identity checks, Accessibility/TCC failures, overlay click-through/non-activating behavior, stop/final cleanup, and no renderer/Playwright control path.
  forbidden_paths: no deleting target mismatch checks entirely; no typing/clicking into the old foreground app; no fake success if activation/focus or native action fails; no broad UI panel; no relying on Playwright/CDP; no patching installed artifacts directly; no hidden unsafe global keystrokes before verifying the target is frontmost/matched.
  expected_implementation_outline: Add a narrow target activation/foregrounding step in the Computer Use manager/native bridge for side-effect actions when target is background/unknown. Prefer focusing the stored target app/window by bundle/pid/window identity, then re-observe and require targetVisibility/frontmost match before native `act`. Record activation attempt/result in policy/trace/evidence. Keep background observe metadata-only and target-bound overlay semantics. Update CLI limitations/docs/skill to remove "must already be frontmost" and describe automatic target activation plus failure modes.
  minimum_regression_matrix: CLI tests prove background target `key/click/type/drag` path attempts activation then executes only after matched frontmost re-observe; activation failure blocks typed before native side effect; wrong foreground remains protected; move still does not move system cursor; click/drag evidence preserved; overlay helper remains non-activating; existing 39 Computer Use/CLI/overlay tests remain passing; Swift parse if native touched; CJS checks; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval. PM will merge and rebuild/restart because this changes real desktop side-effect semantics.

  depends_on: installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:b8f4a51af953f4c276b278f32985a7ef31dbd74925b196a8c7e192aeb59d4100`; PM observed `playwright-cli attach --cdp=http://127.0.0.1:9222` and 9223 connect to Electron websocket but CLI daemon exits after 30s timeout. User confirmed "playwright 链接cdp好像经常超时也看下".
  files: to be scoped by owner; likely `.playwright-cli/` if this repo owns a wrapper/config, project scripts/docs/skills related to self-debug/frontend-debug/playwright-cli, or Root Worker CDP exposure if product-side target handling causes attach stalls.
  base_commit: 9025bc3b591d5bb071fe21a2155ffffd7a43048e
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Diagnose and fix or provide a reliable workaround for frequent `playwright-cli attach --cdp` timeouts against the running Morpheus/Electron Runtime Capsule.
  design_intent: Self-debug should have a dependable attach path to the current running client. If Playwright CLI's daemon/session layer is the failing part, fix the local wrapper/config or document/use a stable fallback; if Morpheus CDP exposure/targets cause the stall, fix product-side CDP target readiness without weakening app runtime semantics.
  current_problem_model: CDP HTTP `/json/version` and `/json/list` on 9222/9223 respond and show Electron UA plus the correct `Root Worker Prototype` renderer URL under the installed capsule. The CLI attach log reaches `<ws connected>` but then daemon exits with a 30s timeout, suggesting a post-connect Playwright attach/session initialization problem, ambiguous multi-port target issue, protocol/Node version issue, or Electron target readiness problem.
  invariants: do not launch/control an external browser as a substitute for current-client self-debug; preserve Electron CDP exposure and app-server runtime; no destructive global npm changes unless explicitly justified and safe; don't hide attach failures by only using curl; any fallback must still inspect the real running renderer.
  forbidden_paths: no switching self-debug docs to `playwright-cli open`; no ignoring the timeout as "not product"; no hardcoding a single transient page id; no patching global installed packages in place without source/rollback; no changing Runtime Capsule restart behavior for this issue unless root cause requires product code.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with current installed CDP and possibly a controlled Electron/dev target. Compare `playwright-cli attach --cdp`, direct Playwright `chromium.connectOverCDP`, and raw CDP `/json`/websocket behavior. Determine whether failure is in the global Playwright CLI version/session daemon, repo `.playwright-cli` config/cache, Node 25 compatibility, multi-port 9222/9223 collision, or Morpheus CDP target exposure. Implement the smallest durable fix: repo wrapper/config/test if in our control, product CDP readiness fix if product-side, or update self-debug/frontend-debug skill docs with a validated fallback if external CLI is the source.
  minimum_regression_matrix: A reproducible attach smoke that can get title/url/console from current Morpheus renderer or a controlled equivalent; proof no external browser is launched; existing frontend/self-debug docs stay accurate; relevant tests/checks for touched code; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval. If product code changes, PM will merge/build/restart/self-debug; docs-only/tooling-only may not need capsule.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered via Runtime Capsule `sha256:bfae2d62fabed52153aa3d28ae3910e0d25de2b73b62650bfc6d8fe0f7d2a6c8` / sourceCommit `eb2e53cb5d56c3a325a55b2005eb1d6074010bf4` after restart request `call_8ZK00huj8lcso01gftUp3Llh`.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST owner_dev_3 delivered commit `6a4f93e5c494f5928d16815ed46dfffdb5e4ec48` with fixed reviewer approval. Root cause: 9222 compatibility proxy exposed an Electron internal empty `type:"page"` target with empty title/url; Playwright auto-attach sent page initialization commands that never responded, causing 30s attach timeout. Fix filters empty page targets in `/json/list`, `Target.getTargets`, and browser websocket target events while preserving valid pages/about:blank/createTarget behavior. Owner validation: remoteDebuggingProxy tests 8 pass; CJS checks pass; `git diff --check` pass; temporary source proxy over installed 9223 direct Playwright smoke pass; Root Worker build pass with existing warnings. PM inspected diff and reran `node --test apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/remoteDebuggingProxy.test.cjs`, CJS checks, and diff check; all passed. PM merged into canonical main as `fa76849419c001cfe2b4d14e2a63f454ef27e115` and reran the same focused validation on main; all passed.

  depends_on: installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:6245accf56a7864020451abc47c51fbb3e2ed5b49ec39872fa284eba30a2a0f1` / sourceCommit `0275c2943bfcb7c28ef97627b50a1cf0f1a77649`; user asked on 2026-09-16 CST to make the remaining Computer Use app-operation abilities work, specifically clicking tabs/buttons and typing, after verifying visible cursor movement in Codex.
  files: likely `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.test.mjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.test.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseMacNative.swift`, README/skill text.
  base_commit: 0275c2943bfcb7c28ef97627b50a1cf0f1a77649
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Make Computer Use CLI support the full app-operation action set by default: `click`, `type`, `key`, and `drag` in addition to `start/observe/move/stop`, so the agent can actually use an app rather than only moving a visual cursor.
  design_intent: User explicitly rejected an extra opt-in / category-block design. In this product model, a `run --actions` request is the operation boundary; action categories should not be hard-blocked just because they are side effects. The target app, not Morpheus UI, is the real operation surface. The implementation must prefer the existing ComputerUseManager/native bridge session boundary over renderer/Playwright shortcuts, and must produce auditable trace/evidence for each real operation.
  current_problem_model: Current CLI blocks `click/type/key/drag` before native side effects via `CLI_DISABLED_ACTIONS`. `ComputerUseManager` already has classification, preflight observe, target mismatch checks, click cursor movement, and a `nativeClient.act` path, but the CLI short-circuits before that path. Native macOS bridge support for click/type/key/drag must be verified and completed if missing.
  invariants: `move` must not move the macOS system cursor. Real `click/type/key/drag` must execute only against the currently frontmost/matched target after preflight and must record trace/policy/evidence. Background targets are not auto-activated and side effects do not run against them; this is a target/environment precondition, not an action-category block. Keep target-bound overlay semantics, helper non-activation, bounded screenshot output, and stop/final hidden overlay state.
  forbidden_paths: no routing through renderer/preload/Playwright/CDP; no fake success if native action did not run; no action-category disabled list for `click/type/key/drag`; no typing/clicking into a background or mismatched app; no automatic activate/bringToFront; no patching installed artifacts directly; no broad UI panel as a substitute for CLI/native contract.
  expected_implementation_outline: Remove the CLI v1 action-category disabling for `click/type/key/drag`. Route all normalized actions through `ComputerUseManager.act`, relying on preflight target/accessibility checks and native execution errors as real blockers. Implement/complete native macOS `act` support for click, type, key with modifiers, and drag. Preserve click/drag cursor path evidence and overlay updates. Update README and `computer-use-cli` skill text to describe full supported action set and the remaining real preconditions (target must be frontmost/matched, permissions required, native failures are surfaced).
  minimum_regression_matrix: Existing 36 Computer Use/overlay tests remain passing. CLI default path executes click/type/key/drag in frontmost matched target with fake native proving native `act` calls. `key Tab` covered. `drag` path/trace/native call covered. Background/mismatch target prevents real native side effects with typed reason. Native unsupported/failure is surfaced as failed/error, not fake success. Click/drag record agent cursor/path evidence. Overlay helper remains non-activating. `stop`/cleanup hidden overlay state remains correct. `node --check`, focused tests, `git diff --check`, owner smoke against a safe app if practical, fixed reviewer approval.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered via Runtime Capsule `sha256:b8f4a51af953f4c276b278f32985a7ef31dbd74925b196a8c7e192aeb59d4100` / sourceCommit `9025bc3b591d5bb071fe21a2155ffffd7a43048e` after restart request `call_Fr9a1leBOgHkWo8rEeep7pFs`.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST PM inspected diff including CLI result mapping, manager policy/drag behavior, Swift native click/type/key/drag path, README, and user skill text. PM reran validation on dev-2 and merged `130bb5c34` into canonical main as `45312e4c0b5cbee7599df0277d7418132e6536c2`; later batched with cursor visual polish merge `9025bc3b591d5bb071fe21a2155ffffd7a43048e`. Main validation: CJS checks pass; Swift parse pass; focused Computer Use/CLI/overlay tests -> 39 passed; `git diff --check` pass. Capsule build/sign passed and installed validation on `b8f4a51a...` confirmed `allowedActions` includes `click/key/type/drag`, `disabledActions=[]`; background `key`/`click` are blocked by target-frontmost `target-mismatch`, not action-category disable. Note: real click/type/drag against user apps was intentionally not executed to avoid unintended side effects; owner covered native action paths with mocked native and Swift parse.

- id: computer-use-cli-visible-cursor
  owner: /self/owner_dev_2
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_2/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2
  branch: fix/computer-use-cli-visible-cursor
  task_type: bugfix/computer-use-cli-visual-feedback
  depends_on: installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:4ca5ef62483638062ebbc3152d09724907ef411460b0d8c2204ed4cd7d01cd18` / sourceCommit `4d15d837a80ce5d5c487a28fe117c8479940eef3`; user reported after safe Computer Use CLI smoke that they did not see the expected cursor. PM reproduced that `node scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs run ... move ...` updates `agentCursor` and pointer path in JSON, but `overlay.visible` remains `false` even for frontmost `com.openai.root-worker-prototype.runtime.dev` because CLI creates `createComputerUseManager()` without an Electron overlay controller. For background targets such as Finder, existing logic intentionally hides overlay with background-target semantics.
  files: likely `scripts/morpheus-computer-use.mjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUse.cjs`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/computerUseOverlay.cjs`, possible new CLI overlay helper script/tests/docs/skill text.
  base_commit: 4d15d837a80ce5d5c487a28fe117c8479940eef3
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Make Computer Use CLI visual cursor behavior match user expectations and reported state. A safe `run` batch with `move` should either show a visible agent cursor/trail when visual overlay is available and appropriate, or return a clear typed limitation that visual overlay is unavailable; it must not silently report successful cursor movement while no visible cursor can appear.
  design_intent: The model-facing CLI should remain the direct Computer Use entrypoint, not a renderer/preload/Playwright control path. Visual feedback is still part of the Computer Use session contract: if the CLI is used interactively and target is frontmost, the user should be able to see the agent cursor/trail, or the CLI must explicitly say why no visual overlay exists. Background target hiding remains valid to avoid drawing over the user's unrelated foreground app. User clarified that target-bound behavior does not need to replay prior path after the user switches to the target app; it is sufficient that the target app can stay in the background without focus stealing, and once the user later switches it frontmost, subsequent real-time move/cursor/path updates are visible.
  current_problem_model: `ComputerUseManager.updateOverlay` only draws when `overlayController` exists and target visibility is frontmost. The Electron app path passes `createComputerUseOverlayController`, but repo CLI uses default `createComputerUseManager()` with no overlay controller, so `updateOverlay` returns before setting `overlay.visible=true`. In smoke, frontmost Root Worker target has `targetVisibility:"frontmost"` but final state still has `overlay.visible:false, reason:null`; background Finder has `targetVisibility:"background"` and hidden overlay by design.
  invariants: `move` must not move macOS system cursor; click/type/key/drag remain disabled in CLI v1; background target overlay remains hidden unless a separately designed explicit "show over foreground" mode exists; do not route CLI actions through renderer/preload/Playwright/CDP; preserve Computer Use safety gates, target visibility semantics, Dock activation policy regular restoration, screenshot cleanup, bounded output, and current JSON schema compatibility as much as possible.
  forbidden_paths: no fake "visible:true" without actual visual overlay; no always-on-top overlay over unrelated foreground app for background targets by default; no moving system cursor to make motion visible; no enabling click/type/key/drag; no dependency on a running renderer or Playwright; no broad RightPanel UI feature; no patching installed artifacts directly.
  expected_implementation_outline: First add a regression proving current CLI frontmost target move completes with cursor path but no visual overlay availability signal. Then implement the smallest robust visual-feedback path. Preferred option is a CLI-owned narrow Electron overlay helper/controller that reuses `computerUseOverlay.cjs` for the lifetime of one `run` process; fallback is a typed `visualOverlay` capability/limitation surfaced by CLI when no overlay controller is available. If implementing helper, ensure it is optional/fails typed, cleans up on stop/finally, preserves app activation policy, and does not require renderer IPC. For background targets, do not draw on the unrelated foreground app and do not promise historical path replay; keep/record target cursor state and make the next observe/move after the target becomes frontmost show real-time cursor/path from that point onward, or state honestly if only next move (not passive delayed watcher) can make it visible.
  minimum_regression_matrix: CLI test for frontmost target with visual overlay available or explicit unavailable limitation; background target still hides overlay with clear reason and no foreground drawing; background->frontmost transition where the subsequent move/observe shows real-time overlay without replaying old path; `move` keeps system cursor unchanged and records agent cursor/path; disabled actions still blocked before side effects; overlay cleanup on stop/finally; Dock activation restoration test if a helper creates windows; `node --check` for touched scripts; focused `computerUse.test.cjs` / `computerUseOverlay.test.cjs`; CLI smoke if practical; `git diff --check`; fixed reviewer approval.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered via Runtime Capsule `sha256:6245accf56a7864020451abc47c51fbb3e2ed5b49ec39872fa284eba30a2a0f1`.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST PM final-reviewed dev-2 cumulative commits through `1dda48de1`, merged into canonical main with merge commit `0275c2943bfcb7c28ef97627b50a1cf0f1a77649`, built full Runtime Capsule, and request `call_2FtuiVBZe3QRjV8cMt9rhBqG` completed. Synced clean dev checkouts dev-2/dev-3 to `0275c2943`.

  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/runtime-restart-recovery-notice-dedupe

  depends_on: installed Runtime Capsule `sha256:0189b0b358c4b5ec5b5548e4472493e3810dc5a9cba38b413e3fda4334924a6e` / sourceCommit `6136bb3ef8ac5a5b77299fe5cbe8dc25b53edc81`; user observed `/self/owner_dev_2` received two identical recovery messages for completed restart request `call_NgZHVF9C4uSemUFbNHpzSuWG`.
  files: to be scoped by owner; likely `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/thread.ts`, `apps/root-worker-prototype/src/lib/conversationPresentation.ts`, related tests, and possibly Rust thread recovery/read replay path if frontend-only dedupe is insufficient.
  base_commit: 6136bb3ef8ac5a5b77299fe5cbe8dc25b53edc81
  status: merged_pending_capsule_delivery
  objective: Ensure a completed Runtime Capsule restart recovery fact is displayed and delivered to a thread at most once per request id, including cases where a live/pending recovery notice and durable thread read/autoresume recovery row both exist.
  design_intent: Treat restart recovery as one durable typed fact keyed by request id / occurrence identity. The fix should dedupe at the typed recovery/display/model-visible merge boundary, not suppress by broad text heuristics or by blocking future legitimate restart requests. The recovery notice may prevent repeated `request_runtime_restart` for the same completed request, but must not create duplicate user-visible or model-visible messages.
  current_problem_model: PM read the durable state for `call_NgZHVF9C4uSemUFbNHpzSuWG`: `runtime-restart-intents.json` has a single `consumed/completed` record and `runtime-autoresume.json` has a single corresponding occurrence. The duplicate in dev-2 therefore likely happens after fact generation, during thread resume/read/display/model-visible merge or live pending notice vs durable replay reconciliation. Existing frontend code has restart recovery notice ids/text indexes and presentation dedupe tests, but the installed behavior still allowed two messages in an owner thread.
  invariants: do not mutate or delete the underlying audit/restart intent records; do not hide unrelated user messages with the same text unless they are typed restart recovery notices for the same request; preserve distinct request ids even if text is identical; preserve command audit output that mentions recovery text; preserve future restart requests after new builds; do not call `request_runtime_restart` for this fix until PM has merged and built a new Capsule.
  forbidden_paths: no string-only global filtering; no one-off special case for `call_NgZHVF9C4uSemUFbNHpzSuWG`; no suppression of all recovery notices; no fake tool output or history rewrite; no UI-only cosmetic hiding if model-visible context can still receive the duplicate.
  expected_implementation_outline: First reproduce or write a focused regression test with a pending/live recovery notice plus durable read/autoresume projection for the same request id in the same target thread. Then fix the narrow merge/projection layer so request-keyed restart recovery facts coalesce atomically across live snapshot, thread read, compact/details, and presentation as applicable. If the duplicate originates backend-side before frontend projection, fix the typed recovery event injection/idempotency boundary there and keep frontend dedupe as a safety net.
  minimum_regression_matrix: focused tests proving duplicate same-request recovery rows collapse to one; distinct request ids remain distinct; same text non-recovery user rows remain; command audit output mentioning recovery remains; existing `thread.test.ts` and `conversationPresentation.test.ts` recovery tests pass; Rust recovery/read replay tests if backend touched; `git diff --check`; reviewer approval.
  pending_capsule_delivery: required after merge because this affects runtime/client recovery display and model-visible context semantics.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST owner_dev_3 delivered `f92c098438e6277f4ac2bb48ff70d8cd459a38c0`; PM accepted and merged as `bde84a1aa39347e4d1017b8998353bf7a0c88f0f`. Root cause: completed local recovery turns skipped item-level restart recovery matching, so durable read/autoresume recovery rows for the same request id could appear again. Fix adds request/text keyed matching before retaining completed local turns.
  next_action: include in next Runtime Capsule build/restart and installed-smoke `/self/owner_dev_2` recovery display.

For v1, support read-only observe plus safe `move` with agent cursor/path evidence; leave real `click/type/key` disabled or policy-blocked unless session/preflight is robust.   design_intent: Computer Use may create a click-through non-focusable overlay for the agent cursor, but that overlay must not mutate or permanently degrade the host app's user-visible activation/Dock semantics. Launcher/manifest/plist were not changed between the last good release and current release.
  invariants: preserve agent-owned cursor overlay/trail, click-through behavior, background target overlay hiding, `move` not moving the system cursor, Computer Use policy/preflight/trace semantics, and existing app-server/Launcher runtime supervision. The fix must not make overlay steal focus or create a Dock/taskbar entry for the overlay window.

  expected_implementation_outline: Pass a narrow macOS app activation adapter into the overlay/controller or Computer Use manager; before/after overlay create/update/destroy/cleanup, ensure host app activation policy is regular and Dock is shown where available. Prefer a small helper that is no-op off macOS or in tests. Add unit coverage using a fake app/dock adapter proving overlay update/destroy/cleanup calls regular restoration while the overlay window remains `focusable:false` and `skipTaskbar:true`.

- id: terminal-command-auto-focus-refresh
  owner: /self/owner_dev_3
  reviewer: /self/owner_dev_3/reviewer
  checkout: /Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3
  branch: fix/terminal-command-focus-refresh
  task_type: bugfix/frontend-electron-terminal
  depends_on: user reported during Computer Use CLI probing that running shell commands / live terminal output can keep refreshing and steal focus from the conversation input; user asked on 2026-09-16 CST to fix it. Depends on main relocation commit `726bf1f02af748c93559c0ec65dfec56cd128aa0`.
  files: to be scoped by owner; likely Root Worker frontend RightPanel/TerminalPanel focus logic and Electron terminal panel state helpers, plus focused tests.
  base_commit: 726bf1f02af748c93559c0ec65dfec56cd128aa0
  pending_sync_from_main: none; dev-3 was fast-forwarded to main `726bf1f02af748c93559c0ec65dfec56cd128aa0` and switched to `fix/terminal-command-focus-refresh`.
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Fix the user-visible behavior where live command/terminal updates repeatedly refresh and/or steal focus from the conversation input. Terminal output should continue updating when visible, but model command activity must not automatically focus/open the Terminal panel unless the user explicitly selects it or initiates an interactive terminal action.
  design_intent: Treat focus as a user-owned UI state, not a side effect of command output arrival. Recent terminal work intentionally made running command output visible and replayable when the user opens it; this bugfix should preserve that capability while preventing background command updates from hijacking focus or causing disruptive panel selection churn.
  current_problem_model: Running `exec_command` / shell probes create live command items and terminal descriptors. Current UI/Electron state appears to update Live Commands/Terminal tabs as output arrives; somewhere in that path it may auto-select/focus the Terminal tab or re-focus its xterm instance on every descriptor/update. This interrupts typing in the conversation input while commands are still running or streaming.
  invariants: preserve live command output streaming, replay on explicit focus, interactive terminal input for user-created PTY sessions, activeCommandItems as RightPanel Live Commands source, and historical command display. Preserve command output and terminal tab state; only change auto-focus/auto-selection behavior. Do not drop output events or hide running commands to avoid focus changes.
  forbidden_paths: do not solve by disabling live command output, removing Terminal panel, hiding Live Commands, suppressing command events, delaying all terminal updates until command exit, or special-casing one command/tool name. Do not make focus depend on brittle DOM timing hacks. Do not start a dev Electron instead of validating installed app later; installed validation must use self-debug/frontend-debug/playwright-cli after capsule delivery if a runtime/frontend change is merged.
  expected_implementation_outline: Audit the path from command/live terminal descriptor updates to RightPanel/TerminalPanel selection and xterm focus. Separate "ensure tab exists/update buffer" from "select/focus terminal". Auto-select/focus should occur only for explicit user actions such as clicking a Live Command, opening Terminal, or interacting with an interactive user shell. Background output updates may mark backgroundActivity but should not steal focus. Add regression tests around descriptor/update handling and/or component focus behavior so a live update does not change the selected/focused terminal unless requested.
  minimum_regression_matrix: focused frontend/Electron tests for TerminalPanel/RightPanel or helper state touched; regression covering background command output update while conversation input/focus is user-owned; regression covering explicit Live Command click still opens/focuses the terminal; `pnpm --dir apps/root-worker-prototype build` if frontend touched; `git diff --check`; reviewer approval. PM will decide Runtime Capsule delivery and use self-debug/frontend-debug/playwright-cli for installed validation after merge.
  pending_capsule_delivery: delivered via Runtime Capsule `sha256:80ef1555244c398e197e291e655652fbdb484446675817b8df0b68434dec5e59` / sourceCommit `a8e934301be37d9c28403eba47ae295fc08d205e` after restart request `call_P5t0vgDAZYcVgkWLJ5xXYtiv`.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST PM merged owner_dev_3 commit `66c3eb657fb1bc37cd63da3ba1006608a8a48bfc` into canonical main as merge commit `a8e934301be37d9c28403eba47ae295fc08d205e`, built/selected Runtime Capsule `sha256:80ef1555244c398e197e291e655652fbdb484446675817b8df0b68434dec5e59`, and installed restart completed via `call_P5t0vgDAZYcVgkWLJ5xXYtiv`. PM accepted the design: terminal output/replay updates no longer call `terminal.focus()` unconditionally; focus now requires explicit focus request tokens from user actions and targeted tab guards, while background command output preserves active tab and marks background activity. This preserves live output/replay and explicit Live Command focus while preventing background output from stealing conversation input focus.
  next_action: no action unless Terminal focus stealing regresses; future polish can add deeper component timing tests.
  blockers: none.
  validation: owner reported component tests `pnpm --dir apps/root-worker-prototype exec tsx --test src/components/TerminalPanel.test.tsx src/components/RightPanel.test.tsx` -> 67 pass, Electron terminal tests `pnpm --dir apps/root-worker-prototype exec node --test electron/terminalPanel.test.cjs` -> 45 pass, Root Worker build -> passed with existing xterm/chunk warnings, and `git diff --check` -> passed. PM manual probe proved `/Applications/ChatGPT.app/Contents/Resources/cua_node/bin/node_repl` can be driven over MCP stdio, `@oai/sky` works inside trusted node_repl, `sky.list_apps()` succeeds, and `sky.get_app_state()` can read the active Root Worker Runtime when the client handles `elicitation/create`; ordinary shell Node import/call fails with trusted-runtime validation.
  files: to be scoped by owner; likely `.morpheus/skills/<computer-use-skill>/`, a CLI/script location chosen by existing project conventions, and possibly docs/tests around CLI invocation. Do not touch runtime command focus behavior in this task.
  base_commit: 33ee138fa7837862feba3f6b49bfc93d96a0886e
  pending_sync_from_main: none; dev-3 was switched to `feature/computer-use-cli-skill` at canonical main `33ee138fa7837862feba3f6b49bfc93d96a0886e`.
  status: relocated_to_user_config
  objective: Produce a small, usable Morpheus-owned Computer Use CLI + skill prototype whose architecture we can evolve ourselves, with read-only MVP commands first and backend/session boundaries that can later support stateful GUI operations.
  design_intent: Build the core shape ourselves because this will likely need product-specific changes later. Open-source macOS automation projects may inform design but should not become the default dependency. The short-term backend may still reuse the already-proven trusted `node_repl` + `@oai/sky` path as an adapter, because it can list apps and read app state today, but CLI/skill structure must not be designed around one private path. The long-term design should allow a self-owned macOS Accessibility/Screenshot/Event backend to replace or coexist with that adapter.
  current_problem_model: `~/.codex` has `computer-use@openai-bundled`, `unified-computer-use@openai-bundled`, `node_repl`, and a bundled `Codex Computer Use.app`. Plain Node can import the `@oai/sky` entry only by absolute path and direct `sky.list_apps()` fails with `Sky Computer Use requires the trusted nodeRepl runtime`. A hand-written MCP stdio client to `node_repl` can expose `js`, import `@oai/sky`, list apps, and read Root Worker app state after responding to `elicitation/create`. Running that through normal `exec_command` is acceptable for command display but not a clean agent-facing UX; the requested product shape is a reusable CLI + skill like `playwright-cli`.
  invariants: preserve current installed Runtime Capsule behavior; do not modify command/live terminal focus behavior in this task; do not require official Codex MCP tools to be model-visible; keep risky Computer Use actions gated by explicit confirmation policy; read-only commands such as `list-apps` and `get-app-state` must not click/type/scroll; do not store sensitive screenshots or AX trees in long-lived repo files; do not depend on a single active Runtime Capsule hash; support full app path to disambiguate duplicate bundle identifiers; keep CLI command layer, session/backend layer, policy/approval layer, and output schema layer separable.
  forbidden_paths: do not adopt an open-source automation project as the core/default dependency for this task; do not bypass macOS/TCC security; do not patch bundled ChatGPT/Codex files under `/Applications/ChatGPT.app` or `~/.codex/plugins/cache`; do not hard-code `b80c404f...` except in notes/tests as a sample; do not implement click/type/paste without a documented confirmation boundary; do not route normal use through a giant inline shell heredoc; do not create extra README/INSTALL docs contrary to skill-creator guidance; do not touch unrelated dirty files or PM progress from the dev checkout.
  expected_implementation_outline: First audit existing project conventions for CLI-like skills (`playwright-cli`, `self-debug`, scripts layout). Then create a small self-owned CLI architecture with explicit command parsing, backend/session abstraction, policy/approval handling, and bounded JSON/text output. MVP should implement at least read-only `list-apps` and `get-app-state --app <name|path|bundle> --json`; the initial backend may be a `node_repl` MCP adapter if that is the fastest stable route, but it must sit behind a replaceable backend boundary and clearly document what would change for a native macOS Accessibility/Screenshot/Event backend. Open-source tools such as macOS-use, Appium Mac2 Driver, Hammerspoon, cliclick, or axcli may be briefly evaluated only as references for API shape/risk, not as the main implementation. Add a concise skill folder `SKILL.md` that tells agents when to use the CLI, how to prefer read-only inspection, how to use full app paths for duplicate bundles, when to request user confirmation, and why stateful GUI操作 later need persistent session/server/repl/batch rather than independent stateless commands.
  minimum_regression_matrix: CLI help/version or usage smoke; `list-apps` read-only smoke; `get-app-state` read-only smoke against a safe app such as active Root Worker or Finder using full app path/name; bounded output behavior; failure path for missing ChatGPT/cua_node or missing approval support; skill validation per `skill-creator` (`quick_validate.py` if available); relevant lint/test/build for touched scripts; `git diff --check`; reviewer approval.
  pending_capsule_delivery: not required; repo-local skill/script prototype only, no Rust/backend/frontend/runtime startup path changes.
  last_update: 2026-09-16 CST user asked to put the skill under user config instead of repo-local. PM installed it at `/Users/bytedance/.morpheus/skills/computer-use-cli`, validated the user-config copy, and removed repo-local `.morpheus/skills/computer-use-cli` from canonical main via commit `726bf1f02af748c93559c0ec65dfec56cd128aa0`. Prior PM merge commit was `5051abf618d73462660bbc8276d6ede3cb888b46`.
  next_action: no runtime restart needed; future work can evolve native backend depth, screenshots, and persistent session/server support from the user-config skill location.

  objective: Make clicking a running Live Commands / model command terminal reliably show the command output transcript in the Terminal panel, while preserving normal interactive shell terminal behavior and live command list behavior.
  design_intent: Live command terminal focus should be a faithful view of the command's current output state. The fix should bind the selected model-command terminal tab to replay/cached output plus subsequent live deltas, not fake a separate UI-only preview or conflate model command tabs with interactive user shell tabs.
  current_problem_model: The click path appears to select or create a command tab (`focusCommandTerminal` / `.terminal-live-command`) and update the RightPanel header, but the visible terminal viewport remains blank. Likely failure points are: active command descriptor lacks `aggregatedOutput`/replay when focus occurs; `commandOutputCache` is not keyed consistently by command id/process id/thread; live pty terminal tabs are treated as read-only tabs without replay content; or React/xterm mount does not write replay bytes for focused model-command tabs.
