  forbidden_paths: do not hide Live Commands entries; do not render command output by parsing conversation card DOM; do not collapse all terminal tabs into one shell session; do not special-case the PM capsule build command text; do not introduce polling loops in renderer when existing terminal state notifications/cache can carry the data.
  expected_implementation_outline: Reproduce with a focused Electron/React test fixture for focusing an active model command with cached/replay output and ensure the terminal panel writes/displays that replay. Inspect `terminalPanel.cjs` functions `focusCommandTerminal`, `commandFocusDescriptorForTerminalFocus`, `activeCommandForTerminalFocus`, `liveCommandSessionForTerminalFocus`, `appendTerminalOutput`, and the RightPanel terminal renderer/mount path. Fix the lowest layer that loses transcript association. Add regression for running command focus showing existing output and continuing to receive appended deltas; add a negative test that an interactive shell tab is unaffected.
  minimum_regression_matrix: running model command with cached/aggregated output -> click Live Commands opens tab and renders output; running model command with no current output but later delta -> delta appears; switching away to shell and back preserves command output; completed command is not listed as live running terminal; multiple active commands do not share output caches; interactive shell still accepts input and displays shell output.

  objective: Diagnose and fix live-thread `thread/read` / conversation boundary behavior so live thread reads preserve compact summary facts and turn/message boundaries, and do not cause unrelated assistant/LLM messages to merge into one display group.
  design_intent: Treat persisted/live thread read output as the source of truth for display boundaries. Do not patch by hiding UI symptoms or assuming compact marker is the only cause. This may come from live `thread/read` merging persisted turns with active turns, compact boundary filtering, or conversation item grouping losing a semantic boundary.   last_update: 2026-09-15 CST owner_dev_2 delivered `a37281510e0e58cfe0f04b5d910e1e6db0129a08` with reviewer approval. Root cause: exact `/self` received both canonical self notice and generic auto-resume for the same expected restart; auto-resume was project-root-only and missed active/waiting child agents; ThreadWatchManager did not retain final/interrupted facts until continuation started, so read/list could show stale active state. Fix: self recovery notice detects typed `userMessage.id`; auto-resume eligibility expands from project roots to active/waiting affected threads while excluding exact `/self` and chat compat; `/self/...` subagents remain eligible; ThreadWatchManager retains final interrupted status until a new turn starts and then clears it. This is a minimum recovery repair, not a full state-machine simplification. PM inspected design boundaries and merged to canonical main as `3abc7803794f24d68ac4bcbf69d8a5ac02976bba`.

This mismatch means the native WebContentsView is larger than the DOM clipping viewport, causing the page to render beyond the visible clipped area; browser page/root scroll metrics are 900/900, so page-level scroll cannot reveal the clipped native-view area.   files: apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/appLifecycle.cjs; apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/appLifecycle.test.cjs; apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/main.cjs; apps/root-worker-prototype/electron/appServer*.cjs; codex-rs/app-server/src/main.rs; codex-rs/app-server/src/message_processor.rs; codex-rs/runtime-launcher/src/process.rs; runtime launcher/app-server focused tests
  base_commit: 091482e01
  pending_sync_from_main: none
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Ensure Runtime Capsule restart gives Electron/app-server/runtime a bounded graceful shutdown path that runs business cleanup and persistence before process-tree termination escalates, while preserving Launcher cooperative supervision and fail-closed rollback semantics; also ensure expected restart recovery prompt fanout and client lifecycle status recovery cover all persisted active root threads, not only `/self`, and do not mis-mark recoverable active threads as completed.
  last_update: 2026-09-14 CST fixed dev-2 as `5735dcb12`, reviewed by `/self/owner_dev_2/reviewer`, fast-forward merged to canonical main, built from canonical main, and installed as Runtime Capsule release `sha256:d358d3a8d135e8f6b9960371e07294a0cd46045c1ad7e43488226654ddaae17f` with manifest sourceCommit `5735dcb12f36d591f24c3d28421edc453f8c1766`. Fix chooses the user-preferred explicit trusted-server graceful shutdown interface: new Host-only `client/lifecycle/shutdown` RPC runs app-server cleanup (`clear_all_thread_listeners`, `drain_background_tasks`, `shutdown_threads`) before `markExpectedRestartHandoffReady()` and `app.exit(75)`, with a 30s Electron timeout/failure preventing normal handoff. It also broadens restart recovery fanout to active/waiting/interrupted/shutdown recoverable root threads, while skipping completed/child/subagent/ephemeral threads, addressing `/self`-only prompt and active-as-completed symptoms.
  next_action: no further action unless the next Runtime Capsule restart still skips recovery prompts for eligible active/waiting/interrupted/shutdown root threads or shows recoverable roots as completed; if reproduced, inspect `thread/list(useStateDbOnly=true)` metadata and `client/lifecycle/shutdown` result for that restart.

  base_commit: 9409fc56a
  pending_sync_from_main: none
  status: installed_effective
  objective: Determine whether reselecting an inactive client thread tab uses only `thread/read`, whether that read subscribes/merges live thread state correctly, and why commandExecution / commandExecutionNotification / activeCommandItems may be missing after restore.
  last_update: 2026-09-13 CST fixed dev-2 as `aa1c1b117`, reviewed by `/self/owner_dev_2/reviewer`, merged to canonical main via merge commit `dca06df88`, built from canonical main, and installed as Runtime Capsule release `sha256:d415b7800832195769ec66a2a97bfa3ae3565d5565adfa0329c460a3783e31b1` with manifest sourceCommit `dca06df8897b03d623166e6c21153ba26597fc7d`. Root cause was backend thread-store `read_thread(include_history=true)`: when a live writer rotated to a compact head, SQLite metadata could still contain an older/stale rollout path; the old read path loaded history from that stale path and missed command items appended after compaction/head rotation. The fix makes include-history reads prefer the live writer rollout head, then overlays SQLite metadata such as title/preview without replacing live history.
  next_action: no further action unless user still sees command items missing after reselecting an inactive live thread tab on installed `dca06df88`; if reproduced, inspect actual `thread/read(include_history=true)` response rollout path/history source first.

  last_update: 2026-09-13 CST user reports TerminalPanel output is still wrong on installed `ca7b14b41` and provided a new screenshot showing `running · fixed size`. Follow-up fixed in dev-3 as `9409fc56a`, reviewed by `/self/owner_dev_3/reviewer`, fast-forward merged to canonical main, built from canonical main, and installed as Runtime Capsule release `sha256:12bbe0f4e9ad8c7a5d535cd33a3699d12317a3bca1af33b9d0ef26cd3bc777d4` with manifest `sourceCommit` `9409fc56a09103f339ed99202c7b66efdacefbdc`; launcher control state selected/active external release matches that capsule, active launch phase is `running`, payload PID `44611`, `lastFailure: None`. Root cause is that running command focus can initially create a read-only model command fallback tab; output deltas continued appending to that fallback, but because a tab existed, the old path never refreshed `terminal/session/list` to upgrade it to the live PTY descriptor. The fix triggers a thread-scoped live session refresh when a running/starting read-only model fallback receives output, then existing merge logic upgrades it to a resizable live PTY and stops refreshing once upgraded.
  last_reopen: 2026-09-13 CST user reports "terminal 还是刚才的问题" after installed `9409fc56a`; user provided screenshot showing Docker/buildkit-style output where subsequent lines begin with a large horizontal indentation. owner_dev_3 determined this matches truncated replay starting from an unknown cursor state: xterm `\n` moves down without returning to column 0, so if replay begins mid-line/progress/wrapped output, later lines inherit the horizontal offset. Fixed in main as `8a4dd2ef6` by starting truncated terminal display replay at a line boundary while preserving raw output and sequence watermarks.
  next_action: no further action unless user still sees TerminalPanel line-start indentation/corruption on installed `d75642d37`; if reproduced, first check whether affected tab is read-only/fallback or live PTY and whether `convertEol`/capability rebuild path applied.

After installed `dca06df88`, user still reported the same line-start indentation. Follow-up root cause: read-only/fallback/cache command output can be LF-only, but TerminalPanel created xterm with `convertEol:false`, so LF moved down without returning to column 0; also fallback->live capability changes were missing from effect deps, so resize/write closures could remain stale. Fixed in `d75642d37` by enabling `convertEol` only for read-only/fixed command views, keeping live/resizable PTY `convertEol:false`, and rebuilding xterm when `canResize/canWrite/readOnlyOutput` changes. - commit: 1b11771f7

Follow-up `2936e03aa` fixed the discovered startup gap: active recovery no longer depends on persisted subscriptions, loaded-thread watch state is initialized from persisted lifecycle status, startup submits a continuation prompt for `Active(Running)`, and `TurnComplete` ignores stale live `Active` snapshots so completed threads do not remain incorrectly active.

## Archived Known Issues Snapshot

## Known Issues
- 用户已确认 `request_runtime_restart` 采用 terminal fire-and-forget control action。成功路径不向当前模型返回普通 FunctionCallOutput，也不等待 Host 向旧 server 回执；server 只做校验和定向投递，Host 收到后先原子持久化 expected-restart intent，再执行 build/update/restart，新进程消费 intent 并进行专用恢复。实现应删除 prepare/commit/output-flush/release 屏障，保留唯一 Host 定向路由、requestId 幂等、same-mode coalesce/cross-mode conflict，并补最小 terminal-tool/runtime 与 expected-restart 恢复语义。
- 2026-09-09 interactive PTY input itself works for both `y\n` and a single `y`, but command notification delivery has a race: when a command emits output or exits immediately after `command_write_stdin`, the output/exit notification can occur before `poll_event` starts waiting. `poll_event` may then time out even though `list_commands` shows the command already finished; adding a short post-input delay makes the typed exit notification arrive normally. Treat this as a command event queue/wakeup issue, not a stdin write failure.
- 2026-09-10 `cargo test -p app-server thread_resume_replays_pending_command_execution_request_approval -- --test-threads=1` hits a 10.24-second `initialize` deadline before target assertions on both canonical main `c8c6742e8` and dev-2 `72d5950c5`. It is a baseline test-environment blocker, not evidence of the Terminal compatibility repair; retain it as a residual validation risk until the harness initialization flake is separately diagnosed.
- 2026-09-03 Right Panel File Preview does not keep OS file descriptors open after reading, but text previews are loaded as full UTF-8 strings into React state and one preview is remembered per project root. There is no explicit file-size cap or preview-memory/LRU cap yet, so very large files or many project roots can still increase renderer memory. Future file preview hardening should add bounded preview size and/or memory eviction without changing the basic read-close file behavior.
- 2026-09-03 editable file preview exposed an installed refresh boundary: ordinary frontend/backend changes can use app-server restart + renderer reload, but changes to Electron `main.cjs` or main-process IPC handlers may require a full Electron app relaunch to enter the running process. This was not the root cause of the missing markdown Edit button after a real quit/open, so it is deferred from the markdown UI follow-up and should be handled as a separate runtime lifecycle task.
- 2026-09-10 `/Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-2` and `/Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev-3` have fast-forwarded to `d10a69268`. `/Users/bytedance/.morpheus/source_workspace-dev` is clean but on an old divergent `4f1bc5e4f` checkpoint and cannot fast-forward; do not assign it new work until its protected legacy branch is explicitly reconciled.
- 2026-09-03 older known issues and undated legacy issues moved to [known-issues-through-2026-08-18.md](pm-progress-archive/known-issues-through-2026-08-18.md).
